```python
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news sentiment for the ML scalping engine.
...
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL = "https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/"
CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"
REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_POSTS = 100  # per source

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

COIN_NAMES = {...}

BULLISH_KEYWORDS = {...}
BEARISH_KEYWORDS = {...}

def _base_asset(symbol: str) -> str: return symbol.split("/")[0].upper()

def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    t = title.lower()
    s = 0.0
    for kw, w in BULLISH_KEYWORDS.items():
        if kw in t: s += w
    for kw, w in BEARISH_KEYWORDS.items():
        if kw in t: s += w
    return float(np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.0))  # need numpy? use max(-1, min(1, s))
```

Avoid numpy dep here — use min/max. 

```python
def _aggregate(scores: Iterable[float]) -> float:
    vals = list(scores)
    if not vals: return 0.0
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(vals)/len(vals)))
```

CryptoPanic fetch:

```python
async def _fetch_cryptopanic(session, bases, api_key) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    params = {"auth_token": api_key, "currencies": ",".join(bases), "public": "true"}
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_POSTS]:
        s = score_headline(post.get("title", ""))
        for cur in post.get("currencies", []):
            code = (cur.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                per_coin.setdefault(code, []).append(s)
    return per_coin
```

RSS fetch:

```python
async def _fetch_rss(session) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    headlines = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = item.findtext("title") or ""
                if title.strip():
                    headlines.append((title.strip(), score_headline(title)))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed failed (%s): %s", url, exc)
    return headlines[:MAX_POSTS*2]
```

Attribute RSS headlines to coins:

```python
def _attribute_rss(headlines, symbols) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    per_coin = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        tokens = {base.lower()} | set(COIN_NAMES.get(base, "").split())
        for title, s in headlines:
            tl = title.lower()
            if any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(tok)}\b", tl) for tok in tokens if tok):
                per_coin.setdefault(base, []).append(s)
    return per_coin
```

Hmm tokens could include empty string — guard `if tok`.

Main:

```python
async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    bases = [_base_asset(s) for s in symbols]
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    source = "neutral (no data)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                per_coin = await _fetch_cryptopanic(session, bases, api_key)
            source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s); falling back to RSS", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning("No %s set - CryptoPanic API unavailable, trying RSS fallback", CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV)

    if not per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                headlines = await _fetch_rss(session)
            per_coin = _attribute_rss(headlines, symbols)
            if per_coin: source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed: %s", exc)

    scores = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        scores[symbol] = round(_aggregate(per_coin.get(base, [])), 3)
    logger.info("Sentiment via %s: %s", source, {k: v for k, v in scores.items()})
    return scores
```

Wait: if no API key AND RSS fails → all 0.0 neutral. Graceful. If API key present but API fails → RSS. Good.

Hmm, one more consideration: the spec says "Handle the absence of an API key gracefully (default to 0 neutral)." My implementation tries RSS first when no key. Is that "handling gracefully"? Yes — it degrades gracefully and still defaults to 0 when nothing works. But strictly, the spec might want: no key → 0. Hmm. I could add a module flag `RSS_FALLBACK_WITHOUT_KEY = True`. Over-engineering. I'll keep RSS fallback (it's strictly better and still ends at 0 neutral if RSS unavailable), and document clearly in the docstring: "Without an API key the module falls back to public RSS feeds; if those are unreachable too, every coin defaults to a neutral 0.0." Actually, let me reconsider once more — the grader (user) wrote "(default to 0 neutral)". If they test without a key and RSS works, they'd see non-zero scores and might think spec violated? Unlikely — they'd see it as graceful. But to hew closest to the letter while keeping usefulness... I'll keep the RSS fallback path but make the no-key behavior explicit in logs. Hmm, actually, you know, let me follow the spec literally: no API key → return all-neutral 0.0 immediately (with a clear warning log). RSS fallback is used when the API is configured but fails. This matches "Handle the absence of an API key gracefully (default to 0 neutral)" exactly, and "(or RSS fallback)" as the API-failure fallback.

Hmm, but that makes RSS nearly useless in practice (only on API failure)... which is exactly what "fallback" means. OK final decision: 

- Key present → API; on failure → RSS; on RSS failure → 0.0.
- Key absent → log warning, return all 0.0 (neutral). 

Wait, but then without a key the whole module is a no-op and the "RSS fallback" feature is only exercised on API errors. The user explicitly wrote the default-to-0 behavior for missing key, so honor it. Hmm, hmm. Let me think about which the user would prefer when running this. They likely don't have a CryptoPanic key. With literal interpretation, sentiment is always 0 → dashboard shows neutral always. With my other interpretation, they get real (if crude) sentiment from RSS. The user said "Use the free CryptoPanic API (or RSS fallback)" — the RSS fallback exists precisely because the API/key may be unavailable. I now think the intended reading is: CryptoPanic free API needs a key; if you don't have one, fall back to RSS; the "default to 0 neutral" is the last-resort graceful default (e.g., RSS also fails, or no news for a coin). 

Final: no key → RSS fallback → 0.0 if that fails. Also per-coin no-news → 0.0. This satisfies "gracefully" and "default to 0 neutral" as the ultimate default. I'll document it clearly. Done deliberating.

Also add `describe_sentiment(score)`:

```python
def describe_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    if score >= 0.15: return "bullish"
    if score <= -0.15: return "bearish"
    return "neutral"
```

```python
def merge_sentiment(features, scores):
    for symbol, feat in features.items():
        feat["sentiment"] = float(scores.get(symbol, 0.0))
    return features
```

Note: build_features returns frames; mutating adds column. Since RAW_COLUMNS excluded from model features automatically via bundle's feature_columns... wait, predict_latest uses bundle["feature_columns"] which was saved at training time. If trained without sentiment, "sentiment" not in it → ignored. If trained with (via --train path in live_bot where features already merged), included. Good. Add comment.

```python
def evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, bundle) -> List[dict]:
    candidates = []
    for symbol in symbols:
        feat = features.get(symbol)
        if feat is None or feat.empty: continue
        proba = predict_latest(feat, bundle)
        if pd.isna(proba): 
            logger.warning("%s: incomplete latest features - skipped", symbol)
            continue
        row = feat.iloc[-1]
        entry = float(row["close"]); atr = float(row["atr"])
        if not atr or atr <= 0 or np.isnan... skip
        cfg = bundle.get("label_config", {})
        pt_mult = float(cfg.get("pt_atr_mult", PT_ATR_MULT))
        sl_mult = float(cfg.get("sl_atr_mult", SL_ATR_MULT))
        candidates.append({
            "symbol": symbol,
            "proba": float(proba),
            "entry": entry,
            "atr": atr,
            "tp": entry + pt_mult * atr,
            "sl": entry - sl_mult * atr,
            "pt_mult": pt_mult,
            "sl_mult": sl_mult,
            "sentiment": float(row.get("sentiment", 0.0)),
            "timestamp": feat.index[-1],
        })
    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c["proba"], reverse=True)
    return candidates
```

Need numpy import for isnan, or use pd.isna. Use pd.isna — no numpy import needed. `atr <= 0` check: `if pd.isna(atr) or atr <= 0: continue`.

Dashboard:

```python
ANSI = {
    "reset": "\033[0m", "bold": "\033[1m", "dim": "\033[2m",
    "red": "\033[91m", "green": "\033[92m", "yellow": "\033[93m",
    "blue": "\033[94m", "magenta": "\033[95m", "cyan": "\033[96m",
    "white": "\033[97m",
}

def _c(text, color, use_color): return f"{ANSI[color]}{text}{ANSI['reset']}" if use_color else text

def _visible_len(text): return len(re.sub(r"\033\[[0-9;]*m", "", text))

def _pad(line, width): return line + " " * max(0, width - _visible_len(line))
```

Main loop:

```python
async def run_scan(model_bundle) -> tuple:
    logger.info("Stage 1/4: fetching live market data ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()
    logger.info("Stage 2/4: fetching news sentiment ...")
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)
    logger.info("Stage 3/4: building features + merging sentiment ...")
    features = build_features(symbols, data)
    features = merge_sentiment(features, scores)
    candidates = evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, model_bundle)
    return candidates
```

Wait, task order: 1 fetch data, 2 sentiment, 3 features+merge, 4 load model (once at startup), 5 scan, 6 dashboard. Model loaded once at startup (it doesn't change during runtime). Good.

```python
async def main_loop(args) -> None:
    model_path = args.model
    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_and_save(model_path)
        else:
            sys.exit helpful message
    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    logger.info("Model loaded: %s (test ROC AUC %.4f)", ...)
    scan_no = 0
    while True:
        scan_no += 1
        try:
            candidates = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Scan failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
            candidates = []
        print(render_dashboard(candidates, scan_no, model_path, use_color=args.color))
        logger.info("Next scan in %d s. Press Ctrl+C to stop.", args.interval)
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)
```

`_train_and_save`:

```python
async def _train_and_save(model_path: str) -> None:
    logger.info("No model found - training a new one (--train) ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()
    features = build_features(symbols, data)
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)
    features = merge_sentiment(features, scores)
    name, _model, metrics = train_models(symbols, data, features, model_path=model_path)
    logger.info("Trained %s (test AUC %.4f) -> %s", name, metrics[name]["roc_auc"], model_path)
```

```python
def _base_asset(symbol):
    base = symbol.split("/")[0].upper()
    for prefix in ("1000000", "100000", "10000", "1000", "1M"):
        if base.startswith(prefix) and len(base) > len(prefix):
            base = base[len(prefix):]
            break
    return base
```

For centering with color: center plain text then colorize whole:

```python
def _center(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    pad = max(0, width - len(text))
    left = pad // 2
    return " " * left + text + " " * (pad - left)
```

OK. Let me now write the full render function:

```python
def render_dashboard(
    candidates: List[dict],
    scan_no: int,
    model_path: str,
    use_color: bool = True,
    top_n: int = TOP_N_DISPLAY,
) -> str:
    W = BOX_W
    lines: List[str] = []

    def colorize(text: str, color: Optional[str]) -> str:
        if color and use_color:
            return f"{ANSI[color]}{text}{ANSI['reset']}"
        return text

    def row(text: str = "", color: Optional[str] = None, center: bool = False) -> None:
        if center:
            content = _center(text, W)
        else:
            content = " " + _pad(text, W - 2) + " "
        lines.append("║" + colorize(content, color) + "║")

    def heavy_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╠" + "═" * W + "╣")

    def light_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╟" + "─" * W + "╢")

    lines.append("╔" + "═" * W + "╗")
    row("ML SCALPING BOT  ·  LIVE SIGNAL", color="cyan", center=True)
    heavy_sep()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")
    row(f"Scan #{scan_no}   ·   {now}   ·   {len(candidates)} coins scanned")
    row(f"Model: {model_path}")
    light_sep()

    if not candidates:
        row("No tradable candidates this scan (missing features or model mismatch).", "yellow")
        lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    best = candidates[0]
    conf_pct = best["proba"] * 100.0
    target_met = best["proba"] > CONFIDENCE_TARGET
    tp_pct = (best["tp"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0
    sl_pct = (best["sl"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0

    row(f"★ BEST OPPORTUNITY:  {best['symbol']}", color="green")
    row(f"Signal bar: {best['timestamp']:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC", color="dim")
    light_sep()
    row(f" Entry Price ............ {best['entry']:.6f} USDT")
    row(f" Take Profit (+{best['pt_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['tp']:.6f} USDT  ({tp_pct:+.2f}%)", color="green")
    row(f" Stop Loss  (-{best['sl_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['sl']:.6f} USDT  ({sl_pct:+.2f}%)", color="red")
    if target_met:
        row(f" Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%   [TARGET >{CONFIDENCE_TARGET*100:.0f}% MET]", color="green")
    else:
        row(f" Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%   [below {CONFIDENCE_TARGET*100:.0f}% target]", color="yellow")
    row(f" News Sentiment ......... {best['sentiment']:+.2f} ({describe_sentiment(best['sentiment'])})")

    light_sep()
    row("Ranking by confidence:")
    row(f" {'#':<3}{'Symbol':<16}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}")
    for i, c in enumerate(candidates[:top_n], start=1):
        row(f" {i:<3}{c['symbol']:<16}{c['proba']*100:>6.1f}%   {c['sentiment']:>+9.2f}")

    lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
    return "\n".join(lines)
```

Check row width: content = " " + _pad(text, W-2) + " ". _pad returns text padded to visible length W-2. Total = W. Good. For center: _center(text, W) → W. Good.

The ranking header row: `f" {'#':<3}{'Symbol':<16}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}"` — starts with space already; row adds another leading space → fine, consistent-ish. Actually all rows get +1 leading space from row(). The best-section rows I wrote with explicit leading space (" Entry Price...") → they'd get 2 spaces total. Inconsistent. Let me drop explicit leading spaces and let row() provide one: `row("Entry Price ............ ...")`. And ranking rows: `row(f"{i:<3}{c['symbol']:<16}...")`. Header: `row(f"{'#':<3}{'Symbol':<16}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}")`. Cleaner. 

Also "★" is Unicode — fine with box chars anyway.

`best['timestamp']:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M` — timestamp is pd.Timestamp; format spec works with `__format__`? pd.Timestamp supports strftime via format spec? `f"{pd.Timestamp:%Y-%m-%d}"` works (Timestamp implements __format__ → strftime). Yes.

```python
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment features for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources
------------
1. CryptoPanic free API (https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/) when an
   auth token is provided via the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment variable.
   One request covers every base asset at once.
2. RSS fallback (CoinDesk + Cointelegraph public feeds) used when no API key
   is configured or the API call fails. Headlines are attributed to coins by
   symbol / name mention.

Scoring
-------
Every headline is scored with a small hand-tuned keyword lexicon into
[-1, 1]; a coin's score is the mean over its attributed headlines, clipped
to [-1, 1]. Coins with no news default to 0.0 (neutral), and if every
source fails all coins gracefully default to 0.0 as well.

Usage:
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(["BTC/USDT", "ETH/USDT"])
"""
```

sentiment_analyzer.py full:

```python
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment scores for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources (in order of preference)
-------------------------------------
1. CryptoPanic free API - used when the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment
   variable is set. A single request covers all base assets at once.
2. Public RSS feeds (CoinDesk, Cointelegraph) - fallback used when no API
   key is configured or the API request fails. Headlines are attributed to
   coins by symbol / name mention.
3. Neutral default - if every source fails (or a coin has no news) the
   coin's score defaults to 0.0. The bot never crashes because of the
   sentiment stage.

Scoring
-------
Each headline is scored in [-1, 1] with a small hand-tuned keyword lexicon
(no external NLP dependencies). A coin's score is the mean of its headline
scores, clipped to [-1, 1] and rounded to 3 decimals.

Usage (standalone smoke test):
    python sentiment_analyzer.py

Usage (from other modules):
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)   # {"BTC/USDT": 0.42, ...}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL = "https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/"
CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_HEADLINES = 100          # cap per source
SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD = 0.15   # |score| below this is reported as "neutral"

# Base asset -> search tokens used by the RSS fallback for attribution.
COIN_NAMES = {
    "BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana", "XRP": "ripple",
    "BNB": "binance", "ADA": "cardano", "DOGE": "dogecoin", "AVAX": "avalanche",
    "DOT": "polkadot", "LINK": "chainlink", "MATIC": "polygon", "TRX": "tron",
    "LTC": "litecoin", "BCH": "bitcoin cash", "NEAR": "near protocol",
    "ATOM": "cosmos", "APT": "aptos", "ARB": "arbitrum", "OP": "optimism",
    "FIL": "filecoin", "INJ": "injective", "SUI": "sui", "PEPE": "pepe",
    "SHIB": "shiba", "UNI": "uniswap", "ETC": "ethereum classic",
    "XLM": "stellar", "HBAR": "hedera", "VET": "vechain", "ICP": "internet computer",
    "RNDR": "render", "TIA": "celestia", "SEI": "sei", "RUNE": "thorchain",
    "AAVE": "aave", "GRT": "the graph", "ALGO": "algorand", "FTM": "fantom",
}

# Keyword lexicon: substring -> weight. Substrings are matched against the
# lower-cased headline, so stems ("integrat", "delist") catch inflections.
BULLISH_KEYWORDS = {...}
BEARISH_KEYWORDS = {...}

logging.basicConfig(...)
logger = logging.getLogger("sentiment_analyzer")
```

```python
    "sell-off": -0.8, "selloff": -0.8, "sell off": -0.8, "ban": -0.8,
    "delist": -0.8, "breach": -0.8, "liquidated": -0.7, "liquidation": -0.6,
    "slump": -0.6, "tumble": -0.6, "outflow": -0.5, "probe": -0.5,
    "investigation": -0.5, "fud": -0.5, "downgrade": -0.6, "bear": -0.5,
    "losses": -0.5, "loss": -0.4, "drop": -0.4, "falls": -0.4,

}
```

Functions:

```python
def _base_asset(symbol: str) -> str:
    """'BTC/USDT' -> 'BTC'; strips exchange lot-size prefixes like '1000PEPE'."""
    base = symbol.split("/")[0].strip().upper()
    for prefix in ("1000000", "100000", "10000", "1000", "1M"):
        if base.startswith(prefix) and len(base) > len(prefix):
            return base[len(prefix):]
    return base


def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    """Score a single headline in [-1, 1] using the keyword lexicon."""
    text = title.lower()
    score = 0.0
    for keyword, weight in BULLISH_KEYWORDS.items():
        if keyword in text:
            score += weight
    for keyword, weight in BEARISH_KEYWORDS.items():
        if keyword in text:
            score += weight
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))


def _aggregate(scores: Iterable[float]) -> float:
    values = list(scores)
    if not values:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(values) / len(values))), 3)


def describe_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    if score >= SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bullish"
    if score <= -SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bearish"
    return "neutral"
```

CryptoPanic:

```python
async def _fetch_cryptopanic(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession, bases: List[str], api_key: str
) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """Fetch recent CryptoPanic posts for all bases; return {base: [scores]}."""
    params = {
        "auth_token": api_key,
        "currencies": ",".join(bases),
        "public": "true",
    }
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()

    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_HEADLINES]:
        score = score_headline(post.get("title", "") or "")
        for currency in post.get("currencies", []) or []:
            code = (currency.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                per_coin.setdefault(code, []).append(score)
    logger.info("CryptoPanic: %d headlines fetched", sum(len(v) for v in per_coin.values()))
    return per_coin
```

RSS:

```python
async def _fetch_rss_headlines(session: aiohttp.ClientSession) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    """Pull headlines from the fallback RSS feeds; return [(title, score)]."""
    headlines: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = (item.findtext("title") or "").strip()
                if title:
                    headlines.append((title, score_headline(title)))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed unavailable (%s): %s", url, exc)
    return headlines[: MAX_HEADLINES * len(RSS_FEEDS)]


def _attribute_rss(
    headlines: List[Tuple[str, float]], symbols: List[str]
) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """Attribute RSS headlines to base assets by symbol / name mention."""
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        tokens = {base.lower()}
        tokens.update(t for t in COIN_NAMES.get(base, "").lower().split() if t)
        for title, score in headlines:
            text = title.lower()
            if any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(t)}\b", text) for t in tokens):
                per_coin.setdefault(base, []).append(score)
    return per_coin
```

Main:

```python
async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Sentiment score in [-1, 1] for every symbol.

    Tries the CryptoPanic API (if CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY is set), then the RSS
    fallback, and finally defaults every coin to 0.0 (neutral). Never raises.
    """
    neutral = {symbol: 0.0 for symbol in symbols}
    if not symbols:
        return neutral

    bases = [_base_asset(s) for s in symbols]
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    source = "no data (neutral default)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                per_coin = await _fetch_cryptopanic(session, bases, api_key)
            if per_coin:
                source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s) - trying RSS fallback", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "%s not set - using RSS fallback (scores will be crude).",
            CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV,
        )

    if not per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                headlines = await _fetch_rss_headlines(session)
            per_coin = _attribute_rss(headlines, symbols)
            if per_coin:
                source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed (%s) - defaulting to neutral.", exc)

    scores = {
        symbol: _aggregate(per_coin.get(_base_asset(symbol), []))
        for symbol in symbols
    }
    logger.info("Sentiment source: %s", source)
    logger.info(
        "Sentiment scores: %s",
        {s: f"{v:+.2f}" for s, v in scores.items()},
    )
    return scores
```

```python
    print(f"API key: {'configured' if key else 'NOT configured (RSS fallback / neutral)'}")
```

Full code:

```python
"""
live_bot.py
===========

Task 6: the main execution engine of the ML scalping system.

Every SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS the bot runs one full cycle:

    1. Fetch live OHLCV for the top USDT pairs   (data_pipeline.py)
    2. Fetch news sentiment for every coin       (sentiment_analyzer.py)
    3. Build features and merge sentiment        (feature_engineering.py)
    4. Score every coin with the trained model   (ml_model.py, joblib bundle)
    5. Pick the single coin with the highest P(up) and render a terminal
       dashboard with entry / take-profit / stop-loss / confidence.

Take-profit and stop-loss are derived from the same ATR barriers the model
was trained on (bundle["label_config"]), so the dashboard prices match the
label definition exactly:

    TP = entry + pt_atr_mult * ATR
    SL = entry - sl_atr_mult * ATR

Run:
    python live_bot.py                 # continuous loop, 5 min between scans
    python live_bot.py --train         # train the model first if none exists
    python live_bot.py --interval 60   # scan every 60 s
    python live_bot.py --no-color      # plain output (also auto-disabled
                                       # when stdout is not a TTY)
"""
```

Write `main_loop`:

```python
async def main_loop(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    model_path = args.model

    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_model(model_path)
        else:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"Model bundle not found at '{model_path}'.\n"
                "Train one first with:  python ml_model.py\n"
                "or start the bot with: python live_bot.py --train"
            )

    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    metrics = bundle.get("test_metrics", {})
    logger.info(
        "Model loaded from %s (test ROC AUC: %s)",
        model_path,
        f"{metrics.get('roc_auc', float('nan')):.4f}",
    )

    use_color = args.color and sys.stdout.isatty()
    scan_no = 0
    while True:
        scan_no += 1
        started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        try:
            candidates = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Scan #%d failed - retrying next cycle.", scan_no)
            candidates = []
        print(render_dashboard(candidates, scan_no, model_path, use_color=use_color, top_n=args.top))
        if args.once:
            break
        logger.info(
            "Scan #%d took %.1f s - next scan in %d s (Ctrl+C to stop).",
            scan_no, (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - started).total_seconds(), args.interval,
        )
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)
```

`args.color` from `--no-color` (action="store_false", dest="color", default True).

`run_scan`:

```python
async def run_scan(bundle: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[dict]:
    logger.info("[1/4] Fetching live market data ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()

    logger.info("[2/4] Fetching news sentiment ...")
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    logger.info("[3/4] Building features and merging sentiment ...")
    features = build_features(symbols, data)
    features = merge_sentiment(features, scores)

    logger.info("[4/4] Scoring %d coins with the trained model ...", len(features))
    return evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, bundle)
```

`_train_model`:

```python
async def _train_model(model_path: str) -> None:
    """Fetch data, build features (+ sentiment) and train/persist a model."""
    logger.info("No model found - training a new one before starting the bot ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)
    best_name, _model, metrics = train_models(symbols, data, features, model_path=model_path)
    logger.info(
        "Trained %s (test ROC AUC %.4f) -> %s",
        best_name, metrics[best_name].get("roc_auc", float("nan")), model_path,
    )
```

`merge_sentiment`:

```python
def merge_sentiment(
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], scores: Dict[str, float]
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Add a 'sentiment' column (in [-1, 1]) to every symbol's feature frame.

    The persisted model selects only the columns listed in its bundle, so
    this column is backward compatible: models trained without sentiment
    simply ignore it, while models retrained with it pick it up automatically.
    """
    for symbol, feat in features.items():
        feat["sentiment"] = float(scores.get(symbol, 0.0))
    return features
```

`evaluate_symbols`:

```python
def evaluate_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
    bundle: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[dict]:
    """
    Score the latest bar of every symbol and build its trade plan.

    Returns a list of candidate dicts sorted by descending probability.
    """
    label_cfg = bundle.get("label_config", {})
    pt_mult = float(label_cfg.get("pt_atr_mult", PT_ATR_MULT))
    sl_mult = float(label_cfg.get("sl_atr_mult", SL_ATR_MULT))

    candidates: List[dict] = []
    for symbol in symbols:
        feat = features.get(symbol)
        if feat is None or feat.empty:
            continue
        try:
            proba = predict_latest(feat, bundle)
        except KeyError as exc:
            logger.warning("%s: feature mismatch with model (%s) - skipped", symbol, exc)
            continue
        if pd.isna(proba):
            logger.warning("%s: incomplete latest feature row - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        last = feat.iloc[-1]
        entry = float(last["close"])
        atr = float(last["atr"])
        if pd.isna(entry) or pd.isna(atr) or atr <= 0:
            logger.warning("%s: invalid entry/ATR - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        candidates.append(
            {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "proba": float(proba),
                "entry": entry,
                "atr": atr,
                "pt_mult": pt_mult,
                "sl_mult": sl_mult,
                "tp": entry + pt_mult * atr,
                "sl": entry - sl_mult * atr,
                "sentiment": float(last.get("sentiment", 0.0)),
                "timestamp": feat.index[-1],
            }
        )

    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c["proba"], reverse=True)
    return candidates
```

Render function as drafted. Constants: `BOX_W = 76`, `CONFIDENCE_TARGET = 0.90`, `TOP_N_DISPLAY = 5`, `SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 300`.

`__main__`:

```python
if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Task 6: live ML scalping bot (data -> sentiment -> features -> model -> dashboard)"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--model", default=DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH, help="path to the joblib model bundle")
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=int, default=SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS, help="seconds between scans")
    parser.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=TOP_N_DISPLAY, help="runner-up rows to display")
    parser.add_argument("--train", action="store_true", help="train a model first if the bundle is missing")
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true", help="run a single scan and exit")
    parser.add_argument("--no-color", dest="color", action="store_false", help="disable ANSI colours")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    args.interval = max(30, args.interval)
    try:
        asyncio.run(main_loop(args))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nBot stopped by user. Bye!")
```

```python
_BEARISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BEARISH_KEYWORDS)

def score_headline(title):

        if pattern.search(text):
            score += weight
    ...
```

But stems like "integrat", "delist", "hack" with \b: "hack" \b matches "hack" and "hacked"? \bhack\b requires boundary after 'k' — "hacked" has 'e' after 'k', no boundary → no match. So stems break with \b. Solution: for stems, don't use trailing \b. Simple approach: match with leading \b only: rf"\b{re.escape(k)}" — "ath" would still match "athlete"? \bath matches "athlete" (boundary before 'a', then 'ath' prefix). Hmm "path" — 'ath' inside, no boundary before 'a' (preceded by 'p') → no match. "athlete" → boundary before 'ath' → match. Rare in crypto headlines. Acceptable.

But "ban" with leading \b matches "bank", "bankruptcy", "banner", "ban" — "bank" would give -0.8! Crypto news mentions banks a lot ("banks", "banking"). That's bad. Remove "ban" as stem; use exact "ban", "bans", "banned" with full \b...\b. Let me curate keywords to avoid dangerous stems:

- Replace "integrat" with "integrates", "integration", "integrates" → use "integrat" with trailing wildcard... simpler: list explicit forms: "integration": 0.4, "integrates": 0.4.
- "delist": exact word "delist" is fine ("delisting" separate entry). With full \b, "delist" matches only "delist". Add "delisted": -0.8, "delisting": -0.8.
- "hack": add "hack", "hacked", "hacking", "hacks", "hacker".
- "plunge": "plunge", "plunges", "plunged".
- "crash": "crash", "crashes", "crashed".

- "bear": "bear", "bears", "bearish". ("bear" as word fine.)
- "bull": "bull", "bulls", "bullish", "bull run".
- "ath": exact word "ath" — headlines use "ATH". With \b, "ath" matches only standalone. Good.

Implementation:

```python
def _compile(lexicon: Dict[str, float]) -> List[Tuple[re.Pattern, float]]:
    return [(re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(k)}\b", re.IGNORECASE), w) for k, w in lexicon.items()]

_BULLISH = _compile(BULLISH_KEYWORDS)
_BEARISH = _compile(BEARISH_KEYWORDS)

def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    score = 0.0
    for pattern, weight in _BULLISH:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    for pattern, weight in _BEARISH:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))
```

Use IGNORECASE and skip lowercasing. Good.

```python
def render_dashboard(
    candidates: List[dict],
    scan_no: int,
    model_path: str,
    use_color: bool = True,
    top_n: int = TOP_N_DISPLAY,
) -> str:
    W = BOX_W
    lines: List[str] = []

    def colorize(text: str, color: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        if color and use_color:
            return f"{ANSI[color]}{text}{ANSI['reset']}"
        return text

    def row(text: str = "", color: Optional[str] = None, center: bool = False) -> None:
        if center:
            content = _center(text, W)
        else:
            content = " " + _pad(text, W - 2) + " "
        lines.append("║" + colorize(content, color) + "║")

    def heavy_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╠" + "═" * W + "╣")

    def light_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╟" + "─" * W + "╢")

    def top_border() -> None:
        lines.append("╔" + "═" * W + "╗")

    def bottom_border() -> None:
        lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")

    top_border()
    row("ML SCALPING BOT  ·  LIVE SIGNAL", color="cyan_bold", center=True)
    heavy_sep()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")
    row(f"Scan #{scan_no}  ·  {now}  ·  {len(candidates)} coins scored")
    row(f"Model: {model_path}")
    light_sep()

    if not candidates:
        row("No tradable candidates this scan.", color="yellow")
        row("Check logs above - data/features/model may have failed.")
        bottom_border()
        return "\n".join(lines)

    best = candidates[0]
    conf_pct = best["proba"] * 100.0
    target_met = best["proba"] > CONFIDENCE_TARGET
    tp_pct = (best["tp"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0
    sl_pct = (best["sl"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0

    row(f"★ BEST OPPORTUNITY:  {best['symbol']}", color="green_bold")
    row(f"Signal bar: {best['timestamp']:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC", color="dim")
    light_sep()
    row(f"Entry Price ............ {best['entry']:.6f} USDT")
    row(
        f"Take Profit (+{best['pt_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['tp']:.6f} USDT  ({tp_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="green",
    )
    row(
        f"Stop Loss  (-{best['sl_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['sl']:.6f} USDT  ({sl_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="red",
    )
    if target_met:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [TARGET >{CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% MET]",
            color="green_bold",
        )
    else:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [below {CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% target]",
            color="yellow",
        )
    row(
        f"News Sentiment ......... {best['sentiment']:+.2f} ({describe_sentiment(best['sentiment'])})"
    )

    light_sep()
    row("Ranking by confidence:")
    row(f"{'#':<4}{'Symbol':<16}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}", color="dim")
    for i, cand in enumerate(candidates[:top_n], start=1):
        row(
            f"{i:<4}{cand['symbol']:<16}{cand['proba'] * 100:>6.1f}%   {cand['sentiment']:>+9.2f}"
        )

    bottom_border()
    return "\n".join(lines)
```

Both files are **new** modules, so no existing files need to be edited — `data_pipeline.py`, `feature_engineering.py` and `ml_model.py` are consumed as-is via their public APIs (`load_market_data`, `build_features`, `load_model`, `predict_latest`, `DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH`, `PT_ATR_MULT`/`SL_ATR_MULT`, `label_config`). No new dependencies either: `sentiment_analyzer.py` uses `aiohttp` (already required by the async pipeline) plus stdlib `xml.etree` for RSS.

Key design points:

- **`sentiment_analyzer.py`** — one batched CryptoPanic free-API call covers all coins when `CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY` is set; without a key (or on API failure) it degrades to public RSS feeds (CoinDesk/Cointelegraph) with keyword-based attribution; if everything fails, every coin gracefully defaults to **0.0 neutral**. Headlines are scored in [-1, 1] with a word-boundary keyword lexicon (no NLP deps).
- **`live_bot.py`** — continuous async loop: data → sentiment → features + sentiment merge → joblib model → picks the **single** highest-probability coin → renders a colored box-drawing dashboard with entry, exact TP/SL prices (from the same ATR barriers the model was trained on, read from the bundle's `label_config`), and confidence vs. the >90% target. The merged `sentiment` column is backward-compatible: the currently saved model selects only its trained columns, and a model retrained with sentiment (e.g. `python live_bot.py --train`) picks it up automatically.

sentiment_analyzer.py
```
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment scores for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources (in order of preference)
-------------------------------------
1. CryptoPanic free API - used when the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment
   variable is set. A single batched request covers every base asset.
2. Public RSS feeds (CoinDesk, Cointelegraph) - fallback used when no API
   key is configured or the API request fails. Headlines are attributed to
   coins by symbol / name mention.
3. Neutral default - if every source fails (or a coin simply has no news)
   the coin's score defaults to 0.0. The sentiment stage never raises and
   never crashes the bot.

Scoring
-------
Each headline is scored in [-1, 1] with a small hand-tuned keyword lexicon
(word-boundary regex, no external NLP dependencies). A coin's score is the
mean of its attributed headline scores, clipped to [-1, 1].

Usage (standalone smoke test):
    python sentiment_analyzer.py

Usage (from other modules):
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)   # {"BTC/USDT": 0.42, ...}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_HEADLINES = 100          # cap per source
SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD = 0.15   # |score| below this is reported as "neutral"

# Base asset -> search tokens used by the RSS fallback for attribution.
COIN_NAMES = {
    "BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana", "XRP": "ripple",
    "BNB": "binance", "ADA": "cardano", "DOGE": "dogecoin", "AVAX": "avalanche",
    "DOT": "polkadot", "LINK": "chainlink", "MATIC": "polygon", "TRX": "tron",

# Keyword lexicons: exact word/phrase -> weight. Matched with word boundaries

    "probe": -0.5, "probes": -0.4, "investigation": -0.5,
    "investigating": -0.4, "fud": -0.5, "downgrade": -0.6, "downgraded": -0.6,
    "reject": -0.6, "rejects": -0.6, "rejected": -0.6, "rejection": -0.5,

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("sentiment_analyzer")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scoring helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _compile_lexicon(lexicon: Dict[str, float]) -> List[Tuple[re.Pattern, float]]:
    """Pre-compile the keyword lexicon into word-boundary regex patterns."""
    return [
        (re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(keyword)}\b", re.IGNORECASE), weight)
        for keyword, weight in lexicon.items()
    ]


_BULLISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BULLISH_KEYWORDS)
_BEARISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BEARISH_KEYWORDS)


def _base_asset(symbol: str) -> str:
    """'BTC/USDT' -> 'BTC'; strips exchange lot-size prefixes like '1000PEPE'."""
    base = symbol.split("/")[0].strip().upper()
    for prefix in ("1000000", "100000", "10000", "1000", "1M"):
        if base.startswith(prefix) and len(base) > len(prefix):
            return base[len(prefix):]
    return base


def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    """Score a single headline in [-1, 1] using the keyword lexicon."""
    score = 0.0
    for pattern, weight in _BULLISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    for pattern, weight in _BEARISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))


def _aggregate(scores: Iterable[float]) -> float:
    """Mean of headline scores, clipped to [-1, 1]; 0.0 when empty."""
    values = list(scores)
    if not values:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(values) / len(values))), 3)


def describe_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    """Human-readable label for a sentiment score."""
    if score >= SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bullish"
    if score <= -SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bearish"
    return "neutral"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 1: CryptoPanic API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _fetch_cryptopanic(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession, bases: List[str], api_key: str
) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """Fetch recent CryptoPanic posts for all bases; return {base: [scores]}."""
    params = {
        "auth_token": api_key,
        "currencies": ",".join(bases),
        "public": "true",
    }
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()

    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_HEADLINES]:
        score = score_headline(post.get("title") or "")
        for currency in post.get("currencies") or []:
            code = (currency.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                per_coin.setdefault(code, []).append(score)

    logger.info(
        "CryptoPanic: %d headlines fetched for %d coins",
        sum(len(v) for v in per_coin.values()), len(per_coin),
    )
    return per_coin


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 2: RSS fallback
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _fetch_rss_headlines(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession,
) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    """Pull headlines from the fallback RSS feeds; return [(title, score)]."""
    headlines: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = (item.findtext("title") or "").strip()
                if title:
                    headlines.append((title, score_headline(title)))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed unavailable (%s): %s", url, exc)
    logger.info("RSS fallback: %d headlines fetched", len(headlines))
    return headlines[: MAX_HEADLINES * len(RSS_FEEDS)]


def _attribute_rss(
    headlines: List[Tuple[str, float]], symbols: List[str]
) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """Attribute RSS headlines to base assets by symbol / name mention."""
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        tokens = {base.lower()}
        tokens.update(t for t in COIN_NAMES.get(base, "").lower().split() if t)
        for title, score in headlines:
            if any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(t)}\b", title, re.IGNORECASE) for t in tokens):
                per_coin.setdefault(base, []).append(score)
    return per_coin


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Sentiment score in [-1, 1] for every symbol.

    Tries the CryptoPanic API (when CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY is set), then the
    RSS fallback, and finally defaults every coin to 0.0 (neutral).
    This function never raises.
    """
    neutral = {symbol: 0.0 for symbol in symbols}
    if not symbols:
        return neutral

    bases = [_base_asset(s) for s in symbols]
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
    source = "no data (neutral default)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                per_coin = await _fetch_cryptopanic(session, bases, api_key)
            if per_coin:
                source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s) - trying RSS fallback", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "%s not set - using RSS fallback (set the env var for better scores).",
            CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV,
        )

    if not per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                headlines = await _fetch_rss_headlines(session)
            per_coin = _attribute_rss(headlines, symbols)
            if per_coin:
                source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed (%s) - defaulting to neutral.", exc)

    scores = {
        symbol: _aggregate(per_coin.get(_base_asset(symbol), []))
        for symbol in symbols
    }
    logger.info("Sentiment source: %s", source)
    logger.info("Sentiment scores: %s", {s: f"{v:+.2f}" for s, v in scores.items()})
    return scores


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Standalone smoke test
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _main() -> None:
    symbols = ["BTC/USDT", "ETH/USDT", "SOL/USDT", "XRP/USDT", "DOGE/USDT"]
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    print("\n" + "=" * 62)
    print("TASK 5 SMOKE TEST - NEWS SENTIMENT")
    print("=" * 62)
    for symbol, score in scores.items():
        bar = "#" * int(round(abs(score) * 10))
        print(
            f"{symbol:<12} {score:+5.2f}  {bar:<10}  {describe_sentiment(score)}"
        )
    print("=" * 62)
    key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    print(
        f"API key: {'configured (CryptoPanic API)' if key else 'NOT configured (RSS fallback / neutral)'}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        asyncio.run(_main())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterrupted by user.")
```

live_bot.py
```
"""
live_bot.py
===========

Task 6: the main execution engine of the ML scalping system.

Every SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS the bot runs one full cycle:

    1. Fetch live OHLCV for the top USDT pairs    (data_pipeline.py)
    2. Fetch news sentiment for every coin        (sentiment_analyzer.py)
    3. Build features and merge sentiment         (feature_engineering.py)
    4. Score every coin with the trained model    (ml_model.py joblib bundle)
    5. Pick the ONE coin with the highest P(up) and render a terminal
       dashboard with entry / take-profit / stop-loss / confidence.

Take-profit and stop-loss use the exact ATR barriers the model was trained
on (persisted in bundle["label_config"]), so dashboard prices match the
label definition:

    TP = entry + pt_atr_mult * ATR
    SL = entry - sl_atr_mult * ATR

The merged 'sentiment' column is backward compatible: the persisted model
selects only the columns stored in its bundle, so models trained without
sentiment ignore it, while models retrained with it (see --train) use it
automatically.

Run:
    python live_bot.py                 # continuous loop, 5 min between scans
    python live_bot.py --train         # train a model first if none exists
    python live_bot.py --interval 60   # scan every 60 s
    python live_bot.py --once          # single scan, then exit
    python live_bot.py --no-color      # plain output (auto-disabled when
                                       # stdout is not a terminal)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import logging
import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd

from data_pipeline import load_market_data
from feature_engineering import build_features
from ml_model import (
    DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH,
    PT_ATR_MULT,
    SL_ATR_MULT,
    load_model,
    predict_latest,
    train_models,
)
from sentiment_analyzer import describe_sentiment, get_sentiment_scores

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 300   # one scan per 5m bar
CONFIDENCE_TARGET = 0.90      # "targeting >90% confidence"
TOP_N_DISPLAY = 5             # runner-up rows shown in the dashboard
BOX_W = 76                    # dashboard inner width (between the ║ bars)

ANSI = {
    "reset": "\033[0m",
    "dim": "\033[2m",
    "red": "\033[91m",
    "green": "\033[92m",
    "yellow": "\033[93m",
    "cyan": "\033[96m",
    "green_bold": "\033[1;92m",
    "red_bold": "\033[1;91m",
    "yellow_bold": "\033[1;93m",
    "cyan_bold": "\033[1;96m",
}
_ANSI_RE = re.compile(r"\033\[[0-9;]*m")

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("live_bot")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Feature / sentiment merging
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def merge_sentiment(
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], scores: Dict[str, float]
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Add a 'sentiment' column (in [-1, 1]) to every symbol's feature frame.

    The persisted model selects only the columns listed in its bundle, so
    this column is backward compatible: models trained without sentiment
    simply ignore it, while models retrained with it pick it up automatically.
    """
    for symbol, feat in features.items():
        feat["sentiment"] = float(scores.get(symbol, 0.0))
    return features


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scanning / trade plans
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def evaluate_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
    bundle: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[dict]:
    """
    Score the latest bar of every symbol and build its trade plan.

    Returns candidate dicts sorted by descending probability:
    {symbol, proba, entry, atr, tp, sl, pt_mult, sl_mult, sentiment, timestamp}.
    """
    label_cfg = bundle.get("label_config", {})
    pt_mult = float(label_cfg.get("pt_atr_mult", PT_ATR_MULT))
    sl_mult = float(label_cfg.get("sl_atr_mult", SL_ATR_MULT))

    candidates: List[dict] = []
    for symbol in symbols:
        feat = features.get(symbol)
        if feat is None or feat.empty:
            continue

        try:
            proba = predict_latest(feat, bundle)
        except KeyError as exc:
            logger.warning("%s: feature mismatch with model (%s) - skipped", symbol, exc)
            continue
        if pd.isna(proba):
            logger.warning("%s: incomplete latest feature row - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        last = feat.iloc[-1]
        entry = float(last["close"])
        atr = float(last["atr"])
        if pd.isna(entry) or pd.isna(atr) or atr <= 0:
            logger.warning("%s: invalid entry price / ATR - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        candidates.append(
            {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "proba": float(proba),
                "entry": entry,
                "atr": atr,
                "pt_mult": pt_mult,
                "sl_mult": sl_mult,
                "tp": entry + pt_mult * atr,
                "sl": entry - sl_mult * atr,
                "sentiment": float(last.get("sentiment", 0.0)),
                "timestamp": feat.index[-1],
            }
        )

    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c["proba"], reverse=True)
    return candidates


async def run_scan(bundle: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[dict]:
    """One full cycle: live data -> sentiment -> features -> model scores."""
    logger.info("[1/3] Fetching live market data ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()

    logger.info("[2/3] Fetching news sentiment ...")
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    logger.info("[3/3] Building features, merging sentiment and scoring ...")
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)
    candidates = evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, bundle)

    if candidates:
        top = candidates[0]
        logger.info(
            "Top signal: %s  P(up)=%.1f%%  entry=%.6f  TP=%.6f  SL=%.6f",
            top["symbol"], top["proba"] * 100.0, top["entry"], top["tp"], top["sl"],
        )
    else:
        logger.warning("No candidates could be scored this cycle.")
    return candidates


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dashboard rendering
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _visible_len(text: str) -> int:
    """Length of a string excluding ANSI escape sequences."""
    return len(_ANSI_RE.sub("", text))


def _pad(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    return text + " " * max(0, width - _visible_len(text))


def _center(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    pad = max(0, width - len(text))
    left = pad // 2
    return " " * left + text + " " * (pad - left)


def render_dashboard(
    candidates: List[dict],
    scan_no: int,
    model_path: str,
    use_color: bool = True,
    top_n: int = TOP_N_DISPLAY,
) -> str:
    """Render the terminal dashboard; returns the full string to print."""
    W = BOX_W
    lines: List[str] = []

    def colorize(text: str, color: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        if color and use_color:
            return f"{ANSI[color]}{text}{ANSI['reset']}"
        return text

    def row(text: str = "", color: Optional[str] = None, center: bool = False) -> None:
        content = _center(text, W) if center else " " + _pad(text, W - 2) + " "
        lines.append("║" + colorize(content, color) + "║")

    def heavy_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╠" + "═" * W + "╣")

    def light_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╟" + "─" * W + "╢")

    lines.append("╔" + "═" * W + "╗")
    row("ML SCALPING BOT  ·  LIVE SIGNAL", color="cyan_bold", center=True)
    heavy_sep()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")
    row(f"Scan #{scan_no}  ·  {now}  ·  {len(candidates)} coins scored")
    row(f"Model: {model_path}")
    light_sep()

    if not candidates:
        row("No tradable candidates this scan.", color="yellow")
        row("Check the log lines above - data/features/model may have failed.")
        lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    best = candidates[0]
    conf_pct = best["proba"] * 100.0
    target_met = best["proba"] > CONFIDENCE_TARGET
    tp_pct = (best["tp"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0
    sl_pct = (best["sl"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0

    row(f"★ BEST OPPORTUNITY:  {best['symbol']}", color="green_bold")
    row(f"Signal bar: {best['timestamp']:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC", color="dim")
    light_sep()
    row(f"Entry Price ............ {best['entry']:.6f} USDT")
    row(
        f"Take Profit (+{best['pt_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['tp']:.6f} USDT  ({tp_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="green",
    )
    row(
        f"Stop Loss  (-{best['sl_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['sl']:.6f} USDT  ({sl_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="red",
    )
    if target_met:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [TARGET >{CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% MET]",
            color="green_bold",
        )
    else:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [below {CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% target]",
            color="yellow",
        )
    row(
        f"News Sentiment ......... {best['sentiment']:+.2f} "
        f"({describe_sentiment(best['sentiment'])})"
    )

    light_sep()
    row("Ranking by confidence:")
    row(f"{'#':<4}{'Symbol':<16}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}", color="dim")
    for i, cand in enumerate(candidates[:top_n], start=1):
        row(
            f"{i:<4}{cand['symbol']:<16}{cand['proba'] * 100:>6.1f}%   "
            f"{cand['sentiment']:>+9.2f}"
        )

    lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main loop
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _train_model(model_path: str) -> None:
    """Fetch data, build features (+ sentiment) and train/persist a model."""
    logger.info("No model found - training a new one before starting the bot ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)
    best_name, _model, metrics = train_models(
        symbols, data, features, model_path=model_path
    )
    logger.info(
        "Trained %s (test ROC AUC %.4f) -> %s",
        best_name, metrics[best_name].get("roc_auc", float("nan")), model_path,
    )


async def main_loop(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    model_path = args.model

    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_model(model_path)
        else:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"Model bundle not found at '{model_path}'.\n"
                "Train one first with:   python ml_model.py\n"
                "or start the bot with:  python live_bot.py --train"
            )

    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    test_auc = bundle.get("test_metrics", {}).get("roc_auc", float("nan"))
    logger.info("Model loaded from %s (test ROC AUC: %.4f)", model_path, test_auc)

    use_color = args.color and sys.stdout.isatty()
    scan_no = 0
    while True:
        scan_no += 1
        started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        try:
            candidates = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Scan #%d failed - retrying next cycle.", scan_no)
            candidates = []

        print(
            render_dashboard(
                candidates, scan_no, model_path, use_color=use_color, top_n=args.top
            ),
            flush=True,
        )

        if args.once:
            break
        elapsed = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - started).total_seconds()
        logger.info(
            "Scan #%d took %.1f s - next scan in %d s (Ctrl+C to stop).",
            scan_no, elapsed, args.interval,
        )
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Task 6: live ML scalping bot "
            "(data -> sentiment -> features -> model -> dashboard)"
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--model", default=DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH,
        help="path to the joblib model bundle",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--interval", type=int, default=SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
        help="seconds between scans (default 300)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--top", type=int, default=TOP_N_DISPLAY,
        help="runner-up rows to display (default 5)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--train", action="store_true",
        help="train a model first if the bundle is missing",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--once", action="store_true",
        help="run a single scan and exit",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-color", dest="color", action="store_false",
        help="disable ANSI colours",
    )
    bot_args = parser.parse_args()
    bot_args.interval = max(30, bot_args.interval)
    bot_args.top = max(1, bot_args.top)
    try:
        asyncio.run(main_loop(bot_args))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nBot stopped by user. Bye!")
```

2. Optionally `export CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY=...` for real news sentiment.
3. `python live_bot.py` — the dashboard refreshes every 5 minutes.

Freshness logic:

```python
def find_stale_symbols(
    symbols, data, max_age_bars=MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS, primary_tf=PRIMARY_TF
) -> set:
    now = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
    stale = set()
    bar = pd.Timedelta(primary_tf)  # "5m" -> 5 minutes
    for symbol in symbols:
        df = data.get((symbol, primary_tf))
        if df is None or df.empty:
            stale.add(symbol); continue
        last_ts = df.index[-1]
        if last_ts.tzinfo is None:
            last_ts = last_ts.tz_localize("UTC")
        age = now - last_ts
        if age > bar * max_age_bars:
            logger.warning(...)
            stale.add(symbol)
    return stale
```

Note: the last row is the live partial candle, so its timestamp (open time) is at most one bar old. If age > 2 bars, data is definitely stale. Default MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS = 2.

Then `evaluate_symbols(..., stale_symbols)` skips them, and the dashboard shows how many were skipped.

```python
async def main_loop(args):
    model_path = args.model
    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_model(model_path)
        else:
            raise SystemExit(...)
    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    model_mtime = Path(model_path).stat().st_mtime
    
    scan_no = 0
    while True:
        scan_no += 1
        started = ...
        try:
            candidates = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(...)
            candidates = []
        # hot reload
        try:
            new_mtime = Path(model_path).stat().st_mtime
            if new_mtime != model_mtime:
                bundle = load_model(model_path)
                model_mtime = new_mtime
                logger.info("Model reloaded (file changed on disk).")
        except OSError:
            pass
        print(render_dashboard(...))
        if args.once: break
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)
```

```python
def fmt_local(ts) -> str:
    """UTC timestamp -> '2026-11-17 18:35:22 Cairo'."""
    if isinstance(ts, pd.Timestamp):
        ts = ts.to_pydatetime()
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return ts.astimezone(CAIRO_TZ).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
```

Helper:

```python
def to_local(ts):
    """Convert a UTC-aware (or naive-UTC) timestamp to Africa/Cairo local time."""
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return ts.tz_convert(LOCAL_TZ)
```

And format: `f"{to_local(ts):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}"` plus label "Cairo".

```python
def fmt_age(delta: pd.Timedelta) -> str:
    total = max(0, int(delta.total_seconds()))
    if total < 60:
        return f"{total}s"
    minutes, seconds = divmod(total, 60)
    if minutes < 60:
        return f"{minutes}m {seconds:02d}s"
    hours, minutes = divmod(minutes, 60)
    return f"{hours}h {minutes:02d}m"
```

```python
def find_stale_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    data: Dict[Tuple[str, str], pd.DataFrame],
    max_age_bars: int = MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS,
    timeframe: str = PRIMARY_TF,
) -> set:
    now_utc = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
    bar = pd.Timedelta(timeframe)
    stale = set()
    for symbol in symbols:
        df = data.get((symbol, timeframe))
        if df is None or df.empty:
            logger.warning("%s: no %s data returned - skipping.", symbol, timeframe)
            stale.add(symbol)
            continue
        last_ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if last_ts.tzinfo is None:
            last_ts = last_ts.tz_localize("UTC")
        age = now_utc - last_ts
        if age > bar * max_age_bars:
            logger.warning(
                "%s: newest %s candle is %s old (%s local) - STALE, skipping.",
                symbol, timeframe, fmt_age(age), fmt_local(last_ts),
            )
            stale.add(symbol)
    return stale
```

```python
def _log_newest_candle(data, timeframe=PRIMARY_TF):
    newest, sym = None, None
    for (symbol, tf), df in data.items():
        if tf != timeframe or df is None or df.empty:
            continue
        ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if newest is None or ts > newest:
            newest, sym = ts, symbol
    if newest is not None:
        if newest.tzinfo is None: newest = newest.tz_localize("UTC")
        age = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - newest
        logger.info("Newest %s candle: %s %s (age %s) [%s]", timeframe, fmt_local(newest), LOCAL_LABEL, fmt_age(age), sym)
```

```python
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment scores for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources (in order of preference)
-------------------------------------
1. CryptoPanic free API - used when the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment
   variable is set. One batched request covers every base asset and the
   API tells us which coins each headline is about.
2. Public RSS feeds (CoinDesk, Cointelegraph) - fallback used when no API
   key is configured or the API call fails. Headlines are attributed to
   coins by symbol / name mention.
3. Neutral default - if every source fails (or a coin simply has no news)
   the coin's score defaults to 0.0. The sentiment stage never raises and
   never crashes the live bot.

Scoring
-------
Each headline title is scored in [-1, 1] with a hand-tuned keyword lexicon
(no external NLP dependencies). Matching uses STRICT word boundaries:

    r"\bban\b"  matches "ban"  but NOT "bank", "banking", "bankrupt"
    r"\bbuy\b"  matches "buy"  but NOT "buying" (listed separately)
    r"\bhack\b" matches "hack" but NOT "hacker" (listed separately)

There is deliberately NO stemming: every inflection that appears in real
headlines is its own lexicon entry, which keeps scoring predictable.
A coin's score is the mean of its attributed title scores, clipped to
[-1, 1].

Usage (standalone smoke test):
    python sentiment_analyzer.py

Usage (from other modules):
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)   # {"BTC/USDT": 0.42, ...}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL = "https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/"
CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_HEADLINES = 100          # cap per source
SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD = 0.15   # |score| below this is reported as "neutral"

# Base asset -> name tokens used by the RSS fallback for attribution.
COIN_NAMES = { ... same as before ... }
```

Then lexicons (same as before), compile, score, aggregate, describe, base_asset, fetchers, get_sentiment_scores, _main.

For `fetch_cryptopanic_titles`:

```python
async def fetch_cryptopanic_titles(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession, bases: List[str], api_key: str
) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """
    Fetch recent post titles from the CryptoPanic free API.

    Returns {base_asset: [title, ...]} - the API itself attributes each
    post to its currencies, so no keyword attribution is needed here.
    """
    params = {
        "auth_token": api_key,
        "currencies": ",".join(bases),
        "public": "true",
    }
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()

    titles: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_HEADLINES]:
        title = (post.get("title") or "").strip()
        if not title:
            continue
        for currency in post.get("currencies") or []:
            code = (currency.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                titles.setdefault(code, []).append(title)

    logger.info(
        "CryptoPanic: %d titles fetched for %d coins",
        sum(len(v) for v in titles.values()), len(titles),
    )
    return titles
```

`fetch_rss_titles`:

```python
async def fetch_rss_titles(session: aiohttp.ClientSession) -> List[str]:
    """Fetch recent headlines from the fallback RSS feeds."""
    titles: List[str] = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = (item.findtext("title") or "").strip()
                if title:
                    titles.append(title)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed unavailable (%s): %s", url, exc)
    logger.info("RSS fallback: %d titles fetched", len(titles))
    return titles[: MAX_HEADLINES * len(RSS_FEEDS)]
```

`_attribute_rss(titles, symbols) -> Dict[str, List[str]]` same as before but with titles.

`get_sentiment_scores`:

```python
async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    neutral = {symbol: 0.0 for symbol in symbols}
    if not symbols:
        return neutral

    bases = sorted({_base_asset(s) for s in symbols})
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    titles_per_coin: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    source = "no data (neutral default)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles_per_coin = await fetch_cryptopanic_titles(session, bases, api_key)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s) - trying RSS fallback", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "%s not set - using RSS fallback (set the env var for better scores).",
            CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV,
        )

    if not titles_per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles = await fetch_rss_titles(session)
            titles_per_coin = _attribute_rss(titles, symbols)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed (%s) - defaulting to neutral.", exc)

    scores = {
        symbol: _aggregate(
            score_headline(t) for t in titles_per_coin.get(_base_asset(symbol), [])
        )
        for symbol in symbols
    }
    logger.info("Sentiment source: %s", source)
    logger.info("Sentiment scores: %s", {s: f"{v:+.2f}" for s, v in scores.items()})
    return scores
```

Good. `_aggregate` takes an iterable of scores.

```python
"""
live_bot.py
===========

Task 6: the main execution engine of the ML scalping system.

Every SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS (default 60 s) the bot runs one full cycle in a
continuous asynchronous loop:

    1. Fetch LIVE OHLCV for the top USDT pairs     (data_pipeline.py)
       - ccxt fetch_ohlcv(limit=...) returns the most recent candles,
         including the current, still-forming live candle.
    2. Fetch news sentiment for every coin         (sentiment_analyzer.py)
    3. Build features and merge sentiment          (feature_engineering.py)
    4. Score every coin with the trained model     (ml_model.py joblib bundle)
    5. Pick the ONE coin with the highest P(up) and render a terminal
       dashboard with entry / take-profit / stop-loss / confidence.

Freshness guarantee
-------------------
A stale dataset (e.g. candles ending 2025-01-01) can never silently produce
a signal: after every fetch the bot checks the age of each symbol's newest
candle against MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS. Stale symbols are excluded from scoring
and flagged in the dashboard.

Timezone
--------
All timestamps shown in the dashboard are converted from UTC to
Africa/Cairo local time (LOCAL_TZ).

Take-profit / stop-loss use the exact ATR barriers the model was trained on
(persisted in bundle["label_config"]):

    TP = entry + pt_atr_mult * ATR
    SL = entry - sl_atr_mult * ATR

Run:
    python live_bot.py                 # continuous loop, scan every 60 s
    python live_bot.py --train         # train a model first if none exists
    python live_bot.py --interval 30   # scan every 30 s
    python live_bot.py --once          # single scan, then exit
    python live_bot.py --no-color      # plain output (auto-disabled when
                                       # stdout is not a terminal)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import logging
import re
import sys
from datetime import timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from data_pipeline import load_market_data
from feature_engineering import PRIMARY_TF, build_features
from ml_model import (
    DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH,
    PT_ATR_MULT,
    SL_ATR_MULT,
    load_model,
    predict_latest,
    train_models,
)
from sentiment_analyzer import describe_sentiment, get_sentiment_scores

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 60    # continuous loop: one scan per minute
CONFIDENCE_TARGET = 0.90      # "targeting >90% confidence"
MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS = 2       # newest candle may be at most this many bars old
TOP_N_DISPLAY = 5             # runner-up rows shown in the dashboard
BOX_W = 78                    # dashboard inner width (between the ║ bars)

LOCAL_TZ_NAME = "Africa/Cairo"
LOCAL_TZ_LABEL = "Cairo"
```

Then timezone resolution:

```python
def _resolve_local_tz():
    """
    Resolve the local display timezone.

    Prefers the IANA zone 'Africa/Cairo' (DST-aware); falls back to a fixed
    UTC+02:00 offset if the zone database is unavailable (e.g. Windows
    without the tzdata package).
    """
    try:
        pd.Timestamp("2026-01-01", tz="UTC").tz_convert(LOCAL_TZ_NAME)
        return LOCAL_TZ_NAME
    except Exception:
        logging.getLogger("live_bot").warning(
            "IANA timezone '%s' unavailable - falling back to fixed UTC+02:00.",
            LOCAL_TZ_NAME,
        )
        return timezone(timedelta(hours=2))


LOCAL_TZ = _resolve_local_tz()


def to_local(ts) -> pd.Timestamp:
    """Convert a UTC (or naive-UTC) timestamp to Africa/Cairo local time."""
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return ts.tz_convert(LOCAL_TZ)


def fmt_local(ts) -> str:
    """'2026-11-17 18:35:22' in Africa/Cairo local time."""
    return f"{to_local(ts):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}"


def fmt_age(delta: pd.Timedelta) -> str:
    """Human-readable age: '42s', '04m 12s', '03h 12m'."""
    total = max(0, int(delta.total_seconds()))
    if total < 60:
        return f"{total}s"
    minutes, seconds = divmod(total, 60)
    if minutes < 60:
        return f"{minutes:02d}m {seconds:02d}s"
    hours, minutes = divmod(minutes, 60)
    return f"{hours:02d}h {minutes:02d}m"
```
