Order: define logging config first, then `_resolve_local_tz()`. Let me put logging.basicConfig near the top before LOCAL_TZ. Actually ANSI and logging config first, then timezone block. Fine.

Freshness:

```python
_TF_MINUTES = {
    "1m": 1, "3m": 3, "5m": 5, "15m": 15, "30m": 30,
    "1h": 60, "2h": 120, "4h": 240, "6h": 360, "12h": 720, "1d": 1440,
}


def _bar_timedelta(timeframe: str) -> pd.Timedelta:
    minutes = _TF_MINUTES.get(timeframe)
    if minutes is not None:
        return pd.Timedelta(minutes=minutes)
    return pd.Timedelta(timeframe)  # pandas parses e.g. '5min', '1h'


def find_stale_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    data: Dict[Tuple[str, str], pd.DataFrame],
    timeframe: str = PRIMARY_TF,
    max_age_bars: int = MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS,
) -> Set[str]:
    """
    Return the symbols whose newest candle is too old to trade on.

    The last row of each frame is the live, still-forming candle, so a
    healthy feed is never more than ~1 bar old. Anything older than
    max_age_bars indicates a stale/historical dataset (e.g. data ending
    2025-01-01) and must not be used for live signals.
    """
    now_utc = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
    max_age = _bar_timedelta(timeframe) * max_age_bars
    stale: Set[str] = set()

    for symbol in symbols:
        df = data.get((symbol, timeframe))
        if df is None or df.empty:
            logger.warning("%s: no %s data returned - marking stale.", symbol, timeframe)
            stale.add(symbol)
            continue
        last_ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if last_ts.tzinfo is None:
            last_ts = last_ts.tz_localize("UTC")
        age = now_utc - last_ts
        if age > max_age:
            logger.warning(
                "%s: newest %s candle is %s old (%s %s local) - STALE, excluded.",
                symbol, timeframe, fmt_age(age), fmt_local(last_ts), LOCAL_TZ_LABEL,
            )
            stale.add(symbol)
    return stale


def log_newest_candle(
    data: Dict[Tuple[str, str], pd.DataFrame], timeframe: str = PRIMARY_TF
) -> None:
    """Log the freshest candle across all symbols (proves real-time data)."""
    newest: Optional[pd.Timestamp] = None
    newest_symbol = ""
    for (symbol, tf), df in data.items():
        if tf != timeframe or df is None or df.empty:
            continue
        ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if newest is None or ts > newest:
            newest, newest_symbol = ts, symbol
    if newest is None:
        logger.warning("No %s data at all - nothing to trade on.", timeframe)
        return
    if newest.tzinfo is None:
        newest = newest.tz_localize("UTC")
    age = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - newest
    logger.info(
        "Newest %s candle: %s %s local (age %s, %s)",
        timeframe, fmt_local(newest), LOCAL_TZ_LABEL, fmt_age(age), newest_symbol,
    )
```

merge_sentiment — same as before.

evaluate_symbols — with stale skip:

```python
def evaluate_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
    bundle: Dict[str, Any],
    stale_symbols: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
) -> List[dict]:
    """
    Score the latest (live) bar of every fresh symbol and build its trade plan.

    Returns candidate dicts sorted by descending probability:
    {symbol, proba, entry, atr, tp, sl, pt_mult, sl_mult, sentiment, timestamp}.
    """
    stale_symbols = stale_symbols or set()
    label_cfg = bundle.get("label_config", {})
    pt_mult = float(label_cfg.get("pt_atr_mult", PT_ATR_MULT))
    sl_mult = float(label_cfg.get("sl_atr_mult", SL_ATR_MULT))

    candidates: List[dict] = []
    for symbol in symbols:
        if symbol in stale_symbols:
            logger.warning("%s: skipped - stale market data.", symbol)
            continue
        feat = features.get(symbol)
        if feat is None or feat.empty:
            continue

        try:
            proba = predict_latest(feat, bundle)
        except KeyError as exc:
            logger.warning("%s: feature mismatch with model (%s) - skipped", symbol, exc)
            continue
        if pd.isna(proba):
            logger.warning("%s: incomplete latest feature row - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        last = feat.iloc[-1]
        entry = float(last["close"])
        atr = float(last["atr"])
        if pd.isna(entry) or pd.isna(atr) or atr <= 0:
            logger.warning("%s: invalid entry price / ATR - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        candidates.append(
            {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "proba": float(proba),
                "entry": entry,
                "atr": atr,
                "pt_mult": pt_mult,
                "sl_mult": sl_mult,
                "tp": entry + pt_mult * atr,
                "sl": entry - sl_mult * atr,
                "sentiment": float(last.get("sentiment", 0.0)),
                "timestamp": feat.index[-1],
            }
        )

    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c["proba"], reverse=True)
    return candidates
```

run_scan:

```python
async def run_scan(bundle: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[List[dict], int]:
    """
    One full cycle: live data -> freshness gate -> sentiment -> features ->
    model scores. Returns (candidates, stale_count).
    """
    logger.info("[1/4] Fetching LIVE market data (most recent candles) ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()

    stale = find_stale_symbols(symbols, data)
    log_newest_candle(data)

    logger.info("[2/4] Fetching news sentiment ...")
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    logger.info("[3/4] Building features and merging sentiment ...")
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)

    logger.info("[4/4] Scoring every coin with the model ...")
    candidates = evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, bundle, stale_symbols=stale)

    if candidates:
        top = candidates[0]
        logger.info(
            "Top signal: %s  P(up)=%.1f%%  entry=%.6f  TP=%.6f  SL=%.6f",
            top["symbol"], top["proba"] * 100.0, top["entry"], top["tp"], top["sl"],
        )
    else:
        logger.warning("No tradable candidates this cycle.")
    return candidates, len(stale)
```

def _utc_age(ts) -> pd.Timedelta:
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - ts

Main loop:

```python
async def main_loop(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    model_path = args.model

    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_model(model_path)
        else:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"Model bundle not found at '{model_path}'.\n"
                "Train one first with:   python ml_model.py\n"
                "or start the bot with:  python live_bot.py --train"
            )

    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    model_mtime = _model_mtime(model_path)
    test_auc = bundle.get("test_metrics", {}).get("roc_auc", float("nan"))
    logger.info("Model loaded from %s (test ROC AUC: %.4f)", model_path, test_auc)
    logger.info(
        "Live bot started - scanning every %d s. Display timezone: %s (%s).",
        args.interval, LOCAL_TZ_NAME, LOCAL_TZ_LABEL,
    )

    use_color = args.color and sys.stdout.isatty()
    scan_no = 0
    while True:                       # continuous asynchronous loop
        scan_no += 1
        started = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
        try:
            candidates, stale_count = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Scan #%d failed - retrying next cycle.", scan_no)
            candidates, stale_count = [], 0

        # Hot-reload the model if it was retrained on disk between scans.
        new_mtime = _model_mtime(model_path)
        if new_mtime is not None and model_mtime is not None and new_mtime != model_mtime:
            try:
                bundle = load_model(model_path)
                model_mtime = new_mtime
                logger.info("Model bundle changed on disk - reloaded.")
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Failed to reload model - keeping previous bundle.")

        print(
            render_dashboard(
                candidates, scan_no, model_path,
                stale_count=stale_count, use_color=use_color, top_n=args.top,
            ),
            flush=True,
        )

        if args.once:
            break

        elapsed = (pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - started).total_seconds()
        logger.info(
            "Scan #%d took %.1f s - next scan in %d s (Ctrl+C to stop).",
            scan_no, elapsed, args.interval,
        )
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)
```

`_model_mtime`:

```python
def _model_mtime(path: str) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        return Path(path).stat().st_mtime
    except OSError:
        return None

Both files are rewritten below. Summary of how your critical requirements are addressed:

**Real-time guarantee (the 2025-01-01 problem)**
- No dates are hardcoded anywhere. Every cycle, `live_bot.py` compares each symbol's newest candle timestamp against the *current* UTC wall clock via a freshness gate (`find_stale_symbols`). A dataset ending 2025-01-01 can never silently produce a "live" signal — stale symbols are logged, excluded from scoring, and flagged in the dashboard.
- `data_pipeline.py` was **not** modified (you listed it as read-only). ccxt's `fetch_ohlcv(limit=N)` *without* a `since` parameter already returns the most recent candles, ending with the current still-forming live candle — which is exactly what the pipeline does. `live_bot.py` now *verifies* this every cycle (newest-candle age is logged and displayed). If you want the assertion hardened inside `data_pipeline.py` itself, say the word and I'll edit it too.

**Timezone** — every timestamp in the dashboard and logs is converted from UTC to **Africa/Cairo** (DST-aware via IANA zone, with a fixed UTC+02:00 fallback if the zone database is missing, e.g. Windows without `tzdata`).

**Loop** — continuous `while True: ... await asyncio.sleep(60)` (default 60 s, configurable). The model bundle is also hot-reloaded if retrained on disk between scans.

**Sentiment lexicon** — strict `\b` word boundaries: `\bban\b` matches "ban" but never "bank"/"bankrupt"; no stemming, each inflection is its own entry.

sentiment_analyzer.py
```
"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment scores for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources (in order of preference)
-------------------------------------
1. CryptoPanic free API - used when the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment
   variable is set. One batched request covers every base asset, and the
   API itself attributes each headline to its coins.
2. Public RSS feeds (CoinDesk, Cointelegraph) - fallback used when no API
   key is configured or the API call fails. Headlines are attributed to
   coins by symbol / name mention.
3. Neutral default - if every source fails (or a coin simply has no news)
   the coin's score defaults to 0.0. The sentiment stage never raises and
   never crashes the live bot.

Scoring
-------
Each headline title is scored in [-1, 1] with a hand-tuned keyword lexicon
(no external NLP dependencies). Matching uses STRICT word boundaries:

    r"\bban\b"  matches "ban"  but NOT "bank", "banking", "bankrupt"
    r"\bbuy\b"  matches "buy"  but NOT "buying" (listed separately)
    r"\bhack\b" matches "hack" but NOT "hacker" (listed separately)

There is deliberately NO stemming: every inflection that appears in real
headlines is its own lexicon entry, which keeps scoring predictable and
dependency-free. A coin's score is the mean of its attributed title
scores, clipped to [-1, 1].

Usage (standalone smoke test):
    python sentiment_analyzer.py

Usage (from other modules):
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)   # {"BTC/USDT": 0.42, ...}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL = "https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/"
CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_HEADLINES = 100          # cap per source
SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD = 0.15   # |score| below this is reported as "neutral"

# Base asset -> name tokens used by the RSS fallback for attribution.
COIN_NAMES = {
    "BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana", "XRP": "ripple",
    "BNB": "binance", "ADA": "cardano", "DOGE": "dogecoin", "AVAX": "avalanche",
    "DOT": "polkadot", "LINK": "chainlink", "MATIC": "polygon", "TRX": "tron",
    "LTC": "litecoin", "BCH": "bitcoin cash", "NEAR": "near protocol",
    "ATOM": "cosmos", "APT": "aptos", "ARB": "arbitrum", "OP": "optimism",
    "FIL": "filecoin", "INJ": "injective", "SUI": "sui", "PEPE": "pepe",
    "SHIB": "shiba", "UNI": "uniswap", "ETC": "ethereum classic",
    "XLM": "stellar", "HBAR": "hedera", "VET": "vechain",
    "ICP": "internet computer", "RNDR": "render", "TIA": "celestia",
    "RUNE": "thorchain", "AAVE": "aave", "ALGO": "algorand", "FTM": "fantom",
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Keyword lexicons
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Every keyword is compiled into r"\b<keyword>\b" (strict word boundaries),
# so partial-word collisions are impossible:
#     "ban"    will NOT fire on "bank", "banking", "bankrupt"
#     "buy"    will NOT fire on "buying"  (listed separately)
#     "hack"   will NOT fire on "hacker"  (listed separately)
# No stemming is performed - each inflection used in real headlines is its
# own entry. Weights are additive within one headline, then clipped.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

BULLISH_KEYWORDS: Dict[str, float] = {
    # strong positive
    "all-time high": 1.0, "record high": 0.9, "new high": 0.6, "ath": 0.6,
    "skyrocket": 0.9, "surge": 0.9, "surges": 0.9, "surged": 0.8,
    "soar": 0.9, "soars": 0.9, "soared": 0.8, "rally": 0.8, "rallies": 0.8,
    "bullish": 0.9, "bull run": 0.8, "breakout": 0.8, "short squeeze": 0.6,
    # moderate positive
    "moon": 0.7, "mooning": 0.7, "pump": 0.6, "pumps": 0.5, "pumping": 0.5,
    "spike": 0.4, "spikes": 0.5, "jump": 0.5, "jumps": 0.6, "jumped": 0.5,
    "climb": 0.4, "climbs": 0.5, "climbing": 0.4, "gain": 0.4, "gains": 0.5,
    "gained": 0.4, "rise": 0.3, "rises": 0.4, "rising": 0.3, "rose": 0.3,
    "rebound": 0.5, "rebounds": 0.5, "recover": 0.4, "recovers": 0.5,
    "recovery": 0.4, "bounce": 0.4, "uptrend": 0.4, "golden cross": 0.4,
    # news / fundamentals
    "approval": 0.8, "approved": 0.8, "approves": 0.6, "green light": 0.6,
    "partnership": 0.6, "partners": 0.4, "adoption": 0.6, "adopts": 0.4,
    "integration": 0.4, "integrates": 0.4, "upgrade": 0.5, "upgrades": 0.4,
    "upgraded": 0.4, "launch": 0.4, "launches": 0.4, "launched": 0.3,
    "inflow": 0.5, "inflows": 0.5, "accumulation": 0.4, "accumulate": 0.4,
    "buy the dip": 0.5, "buy": 0.3, "buys": 0.3, "buying": 0.3, "bought": 0.3,
    "milestone": 0.4, "surpass": 0.5, "surpasses": 0.5, "bull": 0.5, "bulls": 0.4,
}

BEARISH_KEYWORDS: Dict[str, float] = {
    # strong negative
    "crash": -1.0, "crashes": -1.0, "crashed": -0.9, "plunge": -1.0,
    "plunges": -1.0, "plunged": -0.9, "plummet": -1.0, "plummets": -1.0,
    "bankrupt": -1.0, "bankruptcy": -1.0, "insolvency": -0.9, "insolvent": -0.9,
    "rug pull": -1.0, "rugpull": -1.0, "hack": -1.0, "hacked": -1.0,
    "hacks": -0.8, "hacking": -0.8, "hacker": -0.7, "exploit": -0.9,
    "exploited": -0.9, "fraud": -0.9, "ponzi": -0.9, "scam": -0.9,
    # moderate negative
    "bearish": -0.9, "sec charges": -0.9, "sues": -0.8, "sued": -0.7,
    "lawsuit": -0.7, "lawsuits": -0.7, "charged": -0.6, "indicted": -0.8,
    "dump": -0.8, "dumps": -0.7, "dumped": -0.7, "dumping": -0.7,
    "sell-off": -0.8, "selloff": -0.8, "sell off": -0.8, "selling": -0.4,
    "ban": -0.8, "bans": -0.8, "banned": -0.8, "banning": -0.7,
    "delist": -0.8, "delisted": -0.8, "delisting": -0.8,
    "breach": -0.8, "breached": -0.7, "liquidation": -0.6,
    "liquidations": -0.6, "liquidated": -0.7,
    # mild negative
    "slump": -0.6, "slumps": -0.6, "slumped": -0.5, "tumble": -0.6,
    "tumbles": -0.6, "tumbled": -0.5, "outflow": -0.5, "outflows": -0.5,
    "probe": -0.5, "probes": -0.4, "investigation": -0.5,
    "investigating": -0.4, "fud": -0.5, "downgrade": -0.6, "downgraded": -0.6,
    "reject": -0.6, "rejects": -0.6, "rejected": -0.6, "rejection": -0.5,
    "bear": -0.5, "bears": -0.4, "losses": -0.5, "loss": -0.4,
    "drop": -0.4, "drops": -0.4, "dropped": -0.4, "falls": -0.4,
    "fell": -0.4, "falling": -0.3, "decline": -0.4, "declines": -0.4,
    "warning": -0.4, "warns": -0.4, "warned": -0.3, "risk": -0.3,
    "risks": -0.3, "risky": -0.4, "delay": -0.3, "delayed": -0.3,
}

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("sentiment_analyzer")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scoring helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _compile_lexicon(lexicon: Dict[str, float]) -> List[Tuple[re.Pattern, float]]:
    """
    Pre-compile the keyword lexicon into STRICT word-boundary patterns.

    \b...\b guarantees e.g. 'ban' never matches inside 'bank' or
    'bankruptcy', and 'buy' never matches inside 'buying'.
    """
    return [
        (re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(keyword)}\b", re.IGNORECASE), weight)
        for keyword, weight in lexicon.items()
    ]


_BULLISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BULLISH_KEYWORDS)
_BEARISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BEARISH_KEYWORDS)


def _base_asset(symbol: str) -> str:
    """'BTC/USDT' -> 'BTC'; strips exchange lot-size prefixes like '1000PEPE'."""
    base = symbol.split("/")[0].strip().upper()
    for prefix in ("1000000", "100000", "10000", "1000", "1M"):
        if base.startswith(prefix) and len(base) > len(prefix):
            return base[len(prefix):]
    return base


def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    """Score a single headline title in [-1, 1] using the keyword lexicon."""
    score = 0.0
    for pattern, weight in _BULLISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    for pattern, weight in _BEARISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))


def _aggregate(scores: Iterable[float]) -> float:
    """Mean of title scores, clipped to [-1, 1]; 0.0 when empty."""
    values = list(scores)
    if not values:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(values) / len(values))), 3)


def describe_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    """Human-readable label for a sentiment score."""
    if score >= SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bullish"
    if score <= -SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bearish"
    return "neutral"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 1: CryptoPanic free API (async title fetcher)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def fetch_cryptopanic_titles(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession, bases: List[str], api_key: str
) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """
    Fetch recent post titles from the CryptoPanic free API.

    Returns {base_asset: [title, ...]}. The API attributes each post to its
    currencies, so no keyword-based attribution is needed on this path.
    """
    params = {
        "auth_token": api_key,
        "currencies": ",".join(bases),
        "public": "true",
    }
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()

    titles: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_HEADLINES]:
        title = (post.get("title") or "").strip()
        if not title:
            continue
        for currency in post.get("currencies") or []:
            code = (currency.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                titles.setdefault(code, []).append(title)

    logger.info(
        "CryptoPanic: %d titles fetched for %d coins",
        sum(len(v) for v in titles.values()), len(titles),
    )
    return titles


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 2: RSS fallback (async title fetcher + attribution)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def fetch_rss_titles(session: aiohttp.ClientSession) -> List[str]:
    """Fetch recent headlines from the fallback RSS feeds."""
    titles: List[str] = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = (item.findtext("title") or "").strip()
                if title:
                    titles.append(title)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed unavailable (%s): %s", url, exc)
    logger.info("RSS fallback: %d titles fetched", len(titles))
    return titles[: MAX_HEADLINES * len(RSS_FEEDS)]


def _attribute_rss(
    titles: List[str], symbols: List[str]
) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """Attribute RSS titles to base assets by symbol / name mention."""
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        tokens = {base.lower()}
        tokens.update(t for t in COIN_NAMES.get(base, "").lower().split() if t)
        for title in titles:
            if any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(t)}\b", title, re.IGNORECASE) for t in tokens):
                per_coin.setdefault(base, []).append(title)
    return per_coin


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Sentiment score in [-1, 1] for every symbol.

    Tries the CryptoPanic free API (when CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY is set), then
    the RSS fallback, and finally defaults every coin to 0.0 (neutral).
    This function never raises.
    """
    neutral = {symbol: 0.0 for symbol in symbols}
    if not symbols:
        return neutral

    bases = sorted({_base_asset(s) for s in symbols})
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    titles_per_coin: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    source = "no data (neutral default)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles_per_coin = await fetch_cryptopanic_titles(session, bases, api_key)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s) - trying RSS fallback", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "%s not set - using RSS fallback (set the env var for better scores).",
            CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV,
        )

    if not titles_per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles = await fetch_rss_titles(session)
            titles_per_coin = _attribute_rss(titles, symbols)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed (%s) - defaulting to neutral.", exc)

    scores = {
        symbol: _aggregate(
            score_headline(t) for t in titles_per_coin.get(_base_asset(symbol), [])
        )
        for symbol in symbols
    }
    logger.info("Sentiment source: %s", source)
    logger.info("Sentiment scores: %s", {s: f"{v:+.2f}" for s, v in scores.items()})
    return scores


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Standalone smoke test
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _main() -> None:
    symbols = ["BTC/USDT", "ETH/USDT", "SOL/USDT", "XRP/USDT", "DOGE/USDT"]
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    print("\n" + "=" * 62)
    print("TASK 5 SMOKE TEST - NEWS SENTIMENT")
    print("=" * 62)
    for symbol, score in scores.items():
        bar = "#" * int(round(abs(score) * 10))
        print(
            f"{symbol:<12} {score:+5.2f}  {bar:<10}  {describe_sentiment(score)}"
        )
    print("=" * 62)
    key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    print(
        f"API key: {'configured (CryptoPanic API)' if key else 'NOT configured (RSS fallback / neutral)'}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        asyncio.run(_main())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterrupted by user.")

live_bot.py
```
"""
live_bot.py
===========

Task 6: the main execution engine of the ML scalping system.

Every SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS (default 60 s) the bot runs one full cycle in a
continuous asynchronous loop:

    1. Fetch LIVE OHLCV for the top USDT pairs     (data_pipeline.py)
       - ccxt fetch_ohlcv(limit=...) without 'since' returns the most
         recent candles, ending with the current still-forming live candle.
    2. Fetch news sentiment for every coin         (sentiment_analyzer.py)
    3. Build features and merge sentiment          (feature_engineering.py)
    4. Score every coin with the trained model     (ml_model.py joblib bundle)
    5. Pick the ONE coin with the highest P(up) and render a terminal
       dashboard with entry / take-profit / stop-loss / confidence.

Freshness guarantee
-------------------
A stale dataset (e.g. candles ending 2025-01-01) can never silently produce
a signal: after every fetch the bot checks the age of each symbol's newest
candle against MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS. Stale symbols are excluded from scoring
and flagged in the dashboard. No dates are hardcoded - freshness is always
measured against the current UTC wall clock.

Timezone
--------
All timestamps displayed in the dashboard and logs are converted from UTC
to Africa/Cairo local time (LOCAL_TZ, DST-aware).

Take-profit / stop-loss use the exact ATR barriers the model was trained on
(persisted in bundle["label_config"]):

    TP = entry + pt_atr_mult * ATR
    SL = entry - sl_atr_mult * ATR

Run:
    python live_bot.py                 # continuous loop, scan every 60 s
    python live_bot.py --train         # train a model first if none exists
    python live_bot.py --interval 30   # scan every 30 s
    python live_bot.py --once          # single scan, then exit
    python live_bot.py --no-color      # plain output (auto-disabled when
                                       # stdout is not a terminal)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import logging
import re
import sys
from datetime import timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from data_pipeline import load_market_data
from feature_engineering import PRIMARY_TF, build_features
from ml_model import (
    DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH,
    PT_ATR_MULT,
    SL_ATR_MULT,
    load_model,
    predict_latest,
    train_models,
)
from sentiment_analyzer import describe_sentiment, get_sentiment_scores

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 60    # continuous loop: one scan per minute
CONFIDENCE_TARGET = 0.90      # "targeting >90% confidence"
MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS = 2       # newest candle may be at most this many bars old
TOP_N_DISPLAY = 5             # runner-up rows shown in the dashboard
BOX_W = 78                    # dashboard inner width (between the ║ bars)

LOCAL_TZ_NAME = "Africa/Cairo"
LOCAL_TZ_LABEL = "Cairo"

ANSI = {
    "reset": "\033[0m",
    "dim": "\033[2m",
    "red": "\033[91m",
    "green": "\033[92m",
    "yellow": "\033[93m",
    "cyan": "\033[96m",
    "green_bold": "\033[1;92m",
    "red_bold": "\033[1;91m",
    "yellow_bold": "\033[1;93m",
    "cyan_bold": "\033[1;96m",
}
_ANSI_RE = re.compile(r"\033\[[0-9;]*m")

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("live_bot")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Timezone helpers (all display times are Africa/Cairo local)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _resolve_local_tz():
    """
    Resolve the local display timezone.

    Prefers the IANA zone 'Africa/Cairo' (DST-aware); falls back to a fixed
    UTC+02:00 offset if the zone database is unavailable (e.g. Windows
    without the 'tzdata' package).
    """
    try:
        pd.Timestamp("2026-01-01", tz="UTC").tz_convert(LOCAL_TZ_NAME)
        return LOCAL_TZ_NAME
    except Exception:
        logger.warning(
            "IANA timezone '%s' unavailable - falling back to fixed UTC+02:00.",
            LOCAL_TZ_NAME,
        )
        return timezone(timedelta(hours=2))


LOCAL_TZ = _resolve_local_tz()


def to_local(ts) -> pd.Timestamp:
    """Convert a UTC (or naive-UTC) timestamp to Africa/Cairo local time."""
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return ts.tz_convert(LOCAL_TZ)


def fmt_local(ts) -> str:
    """Format a timestamp as 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' in Cairo local time."""
    return f"{to_local(ts):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}"


def _utc_age(ts) -> pd.Timedelta:
    """Age of a timestamp relative to the current UTC wall clock."""
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - ts


def fmt_age(delta: pd.Timedelta) -> str:
    """Human-readable age: '42s', '04m 12s', '03h 12m'."""
    total = max(0, int(delta.total_seconds()))
    if total < 60:
        return f"{total}s"
    minutes, seconds = divmod(total, 60)
    if minutes < 60:
        return f"{minutes:02d}m {seconds:02d}s"
    hours, minutes = divmod(minutes, 60)
    return f"{hours:02d}h {minutes:02d}m"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Freshness gate - stale data must never produce a "live" signal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_TF_MINUTES = {
    "1m": 1, "3m": 3, "5m": 5, "15m": 15, "30m": 30,
    "1h": 60, "2h": 120, "4h": 240, "6h": 360, "12h": 720, "1d": 1440,
}


def _bar_timedelta(timeframe: str) -> pd.Timedelta:
    """Duration of one bar for a Binance-style timeframe string."""
    minutes = _TF_MINUTES.get(timeframe)
    if minutes is not None:
        return pd.Timedelta(minutes=minutes)
    return pd.Timedelta(timeframe)  # pandas parses e.g. '5min', '1h'


def find_stale_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    data: Dict[Tuple[str, str], pd.DataFrame],
    timeframe: str = PRIMARY_TF,
    max_age_bars: int = MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS,
) -> Set[str]:
    """
    Return the symbols whose newest candle is too old to trade on.

    The last row of each frame is the live, still-forming candle, so a
    healthy feed is never more than ~1 bar old. Anything older than
    max_age_bars indicates a stale/historical dataset (e.g. data ending
    2025-01-01) and must not be used for live signals.
    """
    now_utc = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
    max_age = _bar_timedelta(timeframe) * max_age_bars
    stale: Set[str] = set()

    for symbol in symbols:
        df = data.get((symbol, timeframe))
        if df is None or df.empty:
            logger.warning("%s: no %s data returned - marking stale.", symbol, timeframe)
            stale.add(symbol)
            continue
        last_ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if last_ts.tzinfo is None:
            last_ts = last_ts.tz_localize("UTC")
        age = now_utc - last_ts
        if age > max_age:
            logger.warning(
                "%s: newest %s candle is %s old (%s %s local) - STALE, excluded.",
                symbol, timeframe, fmt_age(age), fmt_local(last_ts), LOCAL_TZ_LABEL,
            )
            stale.add(symbol)
    return stale


def log_newest_candle(
    data: Dict[Tuple[str, str], pd.DataFrame], timeframe: str = PRIMARY_TF
) -> None:
    """Log the freshest candle across all symbols (proves real-time data)."""
    newest: Optional[pd.Timestamp] = None
    newest_symbol = ""
    for (symbol, tf), df in data.items():
        if tf != timeframe or df is None or df.empty:
            continue
        ts = pd.Timestamp(df.index[-1])
        if newest is None or ts > newest:
            newest, newest_symbol = ts, symbol
    if newest is None:
        logger.warning("No %s data at all - nothing to trade on.", timeframe)
        return
    if newest.tzinfo is None:
        newest = newest.tz_localize("UTC")
    age = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - newest
    logger.info(
        "Newest %s candle: %s %s local (age %s, %s)",
        timeframe, fmt_local(newest), LOCAL_TZ_LABEL, fmt_age(age), newest_symbol,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Feature / sentiment merging
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def merge_sentiment(
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], scores: Dict[str, float]
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Add a 'sentiment' column (in [-1, 1]) to every symbol's feature frame.

    The persisted model selects only the columns listed in its bundle, so
    this column is backward compatible: models trained without sentiment
    simply ignore it, while models retrained with it pick it up automatically.
    """
    for symbol, feat in features.items():
        feat["sentiment"] = float(scores.get(symbol, 0.0))
    return features


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scanning / trade plans
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def evaluate_symbols(
    symbols: List[str],
    features: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
    bundle: Dict[str, Any],
    stale_symbols: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
) -> List[dict]:
    """
    Score the latest (live) bar of every fresh symbol and build its trade plan.

    Returns candidate dicts sorted by descending probability:
    {symbol, proba, entry, atr, tp, sl, pt_mult, sl_mult, sentiment, timestamp}.
    """
    stale_symbols = stale_symbols or set()
    label_cfg = bundle.get("label_config", {})
    pt_mult = float(label_cfg.get("pt_atr_mult", PT_ATR_MULT))
    sl_mult = float(label_cfg.get("sl_atr_mult", SL_ATR_MULT))

    candidates: List[dict] = []
    for symbol in symbols:
        if symbol in stale_symbols:
            logger.warning("%s: skipped - stale market data.", symbol)
            continue
        feat = features.get(symbol)
        if feat is None or feat.empty:
            continue

        try:
            proba = predict_latest(feat, bundle)
        except KeyError as exc:
            logger.warning("%s: feature mismatch with model (%s) - skipped", symbol, exc)
            continue
        if pd.isna(proba):
            logger.warning("%s: incomplete latest feature row - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        last = feat.iloc[-1]
        entry = float(last["close"])
        atr = float(last["atr"])
        if pd.isna(entry) or pd.isna(atr) or atr <= 0:
            logger.warning("%s: invalid entry price / ATR - skipped", symbol)
            continue

        candidates.append(
            {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "proba": float(proba),
                "entry": entry,
                "atr": atr,
                "pt_mult": pt_mult,
                "sl_mult": sl_mult,
                "tp": entry + pt_mult * atr,
                "sl": entry - sl_mult * atr,
                "sentiment": float(last.get("sentiment", 0.0)),
                "timestamp": feat.index[-1],
            }
        )

    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c["proba"], reverse=True)
    return candidates


async def run_scan(bundle: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[List[dict], int]:
    """
    One full cycle: live data -> freshness gate -> sentiment -> features ->
    model scores. Returns (candidates, stale_count).
    """
    logger.info("[1/4] Fetching LIVE market data (most recent candles) ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()

    stale = find_stale_symbols(symbols, data)
    log_newest_candle(data)

    logger.info("[2/4] Fetching news sentiment ...")
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    logger.info("[3/4] Building features and merging sentiment ...")
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)

    logger.info("[4/4] Scoring every coin with the model ...")
    candidates = evaluate_symbols(symbols, features, bundle, stale_symbols=stale)

    if candidates:
        top = candidates[0]
        logger.info(
            "Top signal: %s  P(up)=%.1f%%  entry=%.6f  TP=%.6f  SL=%.6f",
            top["symbol"], top["proba"] * 100.0, top["entry"], top["tp"], top["sl"],
        )
    else:
        logger.warning("No tradable candidates this cycle.")
    return candidates, len(stale)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dashboard rendering (all times in Africa/Cairo local time)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _visible_len(text: str) -> int:
    """Length of a string excluding ANSI escape sequences."""
    return len(_ANSI_RE.sub("", text))


def _pad(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    return text + " " * max(0, width - _visible_len(text))


def _center(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    pad = max(0, width - len(text))
    left = pad // 2
    return " " * left + text + " " * (pad - left)


def render_dashboard(
    candidates: List[dict],
    scan_no: int,
    model_path: str,
    stale_count: int = 0,
    use_color: bool = True,
    top_n: int = TOP_N_DISPLAY,
) -> str:
    """Render the terminal dashboard; returns the full string to print."""
    W = BOX_W
    lines: List[str] = []

    def colorize(text: str, color: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        if color and use_color:
            return f"{ANSI[color]}{text}{ANSI['reset']}"
        return text

    def row(text: str = "", color: Optional[str] = None, center: bool = False) -> None:
        content = _center(text, W) if center else " " + _pad(text, W - 2) + " "
        lines.append("║" + colorize(content, color) + "║")

    def heavy_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╠" + "═" * W + "╣")

    def light_sep() -> None:
        lines.append("╟" + "─" * W + "╢")

    lines.append("╔" + "═" * W + "╗")
    row(
        f"ML SCALPING BOT  ·  LIVE SIGNAL  ·  {LOCAL_TZ_LABEL} LOCAL TIME",
        color="cyan_bold", center=True,
    )
    heavy_sep()

    now_local = pd.Timestamp.now(tz=LOCAL_TZ)
    header = (
        f"Scan #{scan_no}  ·  {now_local:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S} {LOCAL_TZ_LABEL}"
        f"  ·  {len(candidates)} coins scored"
    )
    if stale_count:
        header += f"  ·  {stale_count} stale skipped"
    row(header)
    row(f"Model: {model_path}")
    light_sep()

    if not candidates:
        if stale_count:
            row(f"{stale_count} symbol(s) excluded: stale market data.", color="yellow")
        row("No tradable candidates this scan.", color="yellow")
        row("Check the log lines above - data/features/model may have failed.")
        lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    best = candidates[0]
    conf_pct = best["proba"] * 100.0
    target_met = best["proba"] > CONFIDENCE_TARGET
    tp_pct = (best["tp"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0
    sl_pct = (best["sl"] / best["entry"] - 1.0) * 100.0
    bar_age = fmt_age(_utc_age(best["timestamp"]))

    row(f"★ BEST OPPORTUNITY:  {best['symbol']}", color="green_bold")
    row(
        f"Signal bar: {fmt_local(best['timestamp'])} {LOCAL_TZ_LABEL}  (age {bar_age})",
        color="dim",
    )
    light_sep()
    row(f"Entry Price ............ {best['entry']:.6f} USDT")
    row(
        f"Take Profit (+{best['pt_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['tp']:.6f} USDT  ({tp_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="green",
    )
    row(
        f"Stop Loss  (-{best['sl_mult']:.1f} ATR) .. {best['sl']:.6f} USDT  ({sl_pct:+.2f}%)",
        color="red",
    )
    if target_met:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [TARGET >{CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% MET]",
            color="green_bold",
        )
    else:
        row(
            f"Confidence ............. {conf_pct:.1f}%  [below {CONFIDENCE_TARGET * 100:.0f}% target]",
            color="yellow",
        )
    row(
        f"News Sentiment ......... {best['sentiment']:+.2f} "
        f"({describe_sentiment(best['sentiment'])})"
    )

    light_sep()
    row("Ranking by confidence:")
    row(
        f"{'#':<4}{'Symbol':<14}{'Prob':>7}   {'Sentiment':>9}   "
        f"{'Bar (' + LOCAL_TZ_LABEL + ')':>11}",
        color="dim",
    )
    for i, cand in enumerate(candidates[:top_n], start=1):
        row(
            f"{i:<4}{cand['symbol']:<14}{cand['proba'] * 100:>6.1f}%   "
            f"{cand['sentiment']:>+9.2f}   {to_local(cand['timestamp']):%H:%M}"
        )

    lines.append("╚" + "═" * W + "╝")
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _model_mtime(path: str) -> Optional[float]:
    """File mtime of the model bundle, or None if unreadable."""
    try:
        return Path(path).stat().st_mtime
    except OSError:
        return None


async def _train_model(model_path: str) -> None:
    """Fetch data, build features (+ sentiment) and train/persist a model."""
    logger.info("No model found - training a new one before starting the bot ...")
    symbols, data = await load_market_data()
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)
    features = merge_sentiment(build_features(symbols, data), scores)
    best_name, _model, metrics = train_models(
        symbols, data, features, model_path=model_path
    )
    logger.info(
        "Trained %s (test ROC AUC %.4f) -> %s",
        best_name, metrics[best_name].get("roc_auc", float("nan")), model_path,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main continuous loop
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def main_loop(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    model_path = args.model

    if not Path(model_path).exists():
        if args.train:
            await _train_model(model_path)
        else:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"Model bundle not found at '{model_path}'.\n"
                "Train one first with:   python ml_model.py\n"
                "or start the bot with:  python live_bot.py --train"
            )

    bundle = load_model(model_path)
    model_mtime = _model_mtime(model_path)
    test_auc = bundle.get("test_metrics", {}).get("roc_auc", float("nan"))
    logger.info("Model loaded from %s (test ROC AUC: %.4f)", model_path, test_auc)
    logger.info(
        "Live bot started - scanning every %d s. Display timezone: %s (%s).",
        args.interval, LOCAL_TZ_NAME, LOCAL_TZ_LABEL,
    )

    use_color = args.color and sys.stdout.isatty()
    scan_no = 0
    while True:                                   # continuous asynchronous loop
        scan_no += 1
        started = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
        try:
            candidates, stale_count = await run_scan(bundle)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Scan #%d failed - retrying next cycle.", scan_no)
            candidates, stale_count = [], 0

        # Hot-reload the model if it was retrained on disk between scans.
        new_mtime = _model_mtime(model_path)
        if (
            new_mtime is not None and model_mtime is not None
            and new_mtime != model_mtime
        ):
            try:
                bundle = load_model(model_path)
                model_mtime = new_mtime
                logger.info("Model bundle changed on disk - reloaded.")
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Failed to reload model - keeping previous bundle.")

        print(
            render_dashboard(
                candidates, scan_no, model_path,
                stale_count=stale_count, use_color=use_color, top_n=args.top,
            ),
            flush=True,
        )

        if args.once:
            break

        elapsed = (pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") - started).total_seconds()
        logger.info(
            "Scan #%d took %.1f s - next scan in %d s (Ctrl+C to stop).",
            scan_no, elapsed, args.interval,
        )
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Task 6: live ML scalping bot "
            "(live data -> sentiment -> features -> model -> dashboard)"
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--model", default=DEFAULT_MODEL_PATH,
        help="path to the joblib model bundle",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--interval", type=int, default=SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
        help="seconds between scans (default 60)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--top", type=int, default=TOP_N_DISPLAY,
        help="runner-up rows to display (default 5)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--train", action="store_true",
        help="train a model first if the bundle is missing",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--once", action="store_true",
        help="run a single scan and exit",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-color", dest="color", action="store_false",
        help="disable ANSI colours",
    )
    bot_args = parser.parse_args()
    bot_args.interval = max(10, bot_args.interval)
    bot_args.top = max(1, bot_args.top)
    try:
        asyncio.run(main_loop(bot_args))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nBot stopped by user. Bye!")
```

Run it with:

1. `python ml_model.py` (or `python live_bot.py --train`) — creates `models/scalp_model.joblib`.
2. `python live_bot.py` — scans every 60 s; all times shown in Cairo local time.

One note: on Windows, `pip install tzdata` gives full DST-aware Cairo time; without it the bot automatically falls back to fixed UTC+02:00 (which is correct for Cairo in November anyway).

Both files are complete below. How each critical requirement is met:

**`live_bot.py`**
1. **Strict real-time** — no dates are hardcoded anywhere. Every cycle fetches fresh candles via `load_market_data()` (ccxt `fetch_ohlcv(limit=...)` without `since` returns the most recent candles, ending with the current still-forming live candle), then verifies freshness against the *current UTC wall clock* — so "today" is always the machine's actual date (Nov 17, 2026 on your system). The newest-candle age is logged and shown in the dashboard.
2. **Continuous async loop** — `while True:` → full scan → `await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)` (default 60 s). The model bundle is hot-reloaded if retrained on disk between scans.
3. **Africa/Cairo timezone** — every displayed timestamp (header clock, signal bar, ranking rows) is converted from UTC to Cairo via IANA `Africa/Cairo` (DST-aware), with a fixed UTC+02:00 fallback if the tz database is missing.
4. **Stale-data gate** — `find_stale_symbols()` excludes any symbol whose newest candle is older than `MAX_CANDLE_AGE_BARS` (default 2 bars = 10 min for 5m). Stale symbols are logged, skipped in scoring, and counted in the dashboard header — a dataset ending 2025-01-01 can never produce a signal.

**`sentiment_analyzer.py`**
1. **Strict `\b` boundaries** — every keyword is compiled to `r"\b<keyword>\b"`: `\bban\b` never matches "bank"/"bankruptcy", `\bhack\b` never matches "hacker". No stemming — each inflection is its own entry.
2. **CryptoPanic → RSS → neutral** — async `fetch_cryptopanic_titles()` when `CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY` is set; async `fetch_rss_titles()` fallback (CoinDesk/Cointelegraph) with keyword attribution; if everything fails, every coin defaults to **0.0**. The module never raises.

No changes are needed to `data_pipeline.py`, `feature_engineering.py`, `ml_model.py`, or `requirements.txt` — all are consumed as-is via their public APIs. Since you confirmed the model is already saved, `python live_bot.py` runs immediately.

r"""
sentiment_analyzer.py
=====================

Task 5: news-based sentiment scores for the ML scalping engine.

Data sources (in order of preference)
-------------------------------------
1. CryptoPanic free API - used when the CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY environment
   variable is set. One batched request covers every base asset, and the
   API itself attributes each headline to its coins.
2. Public RSS feeds (CoinDesk, Cointelegraph) - fallback used when no API
   key is configured or the API call fails. Headlines are attributed to
   coins by symbol / name mention.
3. Neutral default - if every source fails (or a coin simply has no news)
   the coin's score defaults to 0.0. The sentiment stage never raises and
   never crashes the live bot.

Scoring
-------
Each headline title is scored in [-1, 1] with a hand-tuned keyword lexicon
(no external NLP dependencies). Matching uses STRICT word boundaries:

    r"\bban\b"  matches "ban"  but NOT "bank", "banking", "bankrupt"
    r"\bbuy\b"  matches "buy"  but NOT "buying" (listed separately)
    r"\bhack\b" matches "hack" but NOT "hacker" (listed separately)

There is deliberately NO stemming: every inflection that appears in real
headlines is its own lexicon entry, which keeps scoring predictable and
dependency-free. A coin's score is the mean of its attributed title
scores, clipped to [-1, 1].

Usage (standalone smoke test):
    python sentiment_analyzer.py

Usage (from other modules):
    from sentiment_analyzer import get_sentiment_scores
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)   # {"BTC/USDT": 0.42, ...}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import aiohttp

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL = "https://cryptopanic.com/api/free/v1/posts/"
CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV = "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY"

RSS_FEEDS = (
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15)
MAX_HEADLINES = 100          # cap per source
SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD = 0.15   # |score| below this is reported as "neutral"

# Base asset -> name tokens used by the RSS fallback for attribution.
COIN_NAMES = {
    "BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana", "XRP": "ripple",
    "BNB": "binance", "ADA": "cardano", "DOGE": "dogecoin", "AVAX": "avalanche",
    "DOT": "polkadot", "LINK": "chainlink", "MATIC": "polygon", "TRX": "tron",
    "LTC": "litecoin", "BCH": "bitcoin cash", "NEAR": "near protocol",
    "ATOM": "cosmos", "APT": "aptos", "ARB": "arbitrum", "OP": "optimism",
    "FIL": "filecoin", "INJ": "injective", "SUI": "sui", "PEPE": "pepe",
    "SHIB": "shiba", "UNI": "uniswap", "ETC": "ethereum classic",
    "XLM": "stellar", "HBAR": "hedera", "VET": "vechain",
    "ICP": "internet computer", "RNDR": "render", "TIA": "celestia",
    "RUNE": "thorchain", "AAVE": "aave", "ALGO": "algorand", "FTM": "fantom",
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Keyword lexicons
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Every keyword is compiled into r"\b<keyword>\b" (strict word boundaries),
# so partial-word collisions are impossible:
#     "ban"    will NOT fire on "bank", "banking", "bankrupt"
#     "buy"    will NOT fire on "buying"  (listed separately)
#     "hack"   will NOT fire on "hacker"  (listed separately)
# No stemming is performed - each inflection used in real headlines is its
# own entry. Weights are additive within one headline, then clipped.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

BULLISH_KEYWORDS: Dict[str, float] = {
    # strong positive
    "all-time high": 1.0, "record high": 0.9, "new high": 0.6, "ath": 0.6,
    "skyrocket": 0.9, "surge": 0.9, "surges": 0.9, "surged": 0.8,
    "soar": 0.9, "soars": 0.9, "soared": 0.8, "rally": 0.8, "rallies": 0.8,
    "bullish": 0.9, "bull run": 0.8, "breakout": 0.8, "short squeeze": 0.6,
    # moderate positive
    "moon": 0.7, "mooning": 0.7, "pump": 0.6, "pumps": 0.5, "pumping": 0.5,
    "spike": 0.4, "spikes": 0.5, "jump": 0.5, "jumps": 0.6, "jumped": 0.5,
    "climb": 0.4, "climbs": 0.5, "climbing": 0.4, "gain": 0.4, "gains": 0.5,
    "gained": 0.4, "rise": 0.3, "rises": 0.4, "rising": 0.3, "rose": 0.3,
    "rebound": 0.5, "rebounds": 0.5, "recover": 0.4, "recovers": 0.5,
    "recovery": 0.4, "bounce": 0.4, "uptrend": 0.4, "golden cross": 0.4,
    # news / fundamentals
    "approval": 0.8, "approved": 0.8, "approves": 0.6, "green light": 0.6,
    "partnership": 0.6, "partners": 0.4, "adoption": 0.6, "adopts": 0.4,
    "integration": 0.4, "integrates": 0.4, "upgrade": 0.5, "upgrades": 0.4,
    "upgraded": 0.4, "launch": 0.4, "launches": 0.4, "launched": 0.3,
    "inflow": 0.5, "inflows": 0.5, "accumulation": 0.4, "accumulate": 0.4,
    "buy the dip": 0.5, "buy": 0.3, "buys": 0.3, "buying": 0.3, "bought": 0.3,
    "milestone": 0.4, "surpass": 0.5, "surpasses": 0.5, "bull": 0.5, "bulls": 0.4,
}

BEARISH_KEYWORDS: Dict[str, float] = {
    # strong negative
    "crash": -1.0, "crashes": -1.0, "crashed": -0.9, "plunge": -1.0,
    "plunges": -1.0, "plunged": -0.9, "plummet": -1.0, "plummets": -1.0,
    "bankrupt": -1.0, "bankruptcy": -1.0, "insolvency": -0.9, "insolvent": -0.9,
    "rug pull": -1.0, "rugpull": -1.0, "hack": -1.0, "hacked": -1.0,
    "hacks": -0.8, "hacking": -0.8, "hacker": -0.7, "exploit": -0.9,
    "exploited": -0.9, "fraud": -0.9, "ponzi": -0.9, "scam": -0.9,
    # moderate negative
    "bearish": -0.9, "sec charges": -0.9, "sues": -0.8, "sued": -0.7,
    "lawsuit": -0.7, "lawsuits": -0.7, "charged": -0.6, "indicted": -0.8,
    "dump": -0.8, "dumps": -0.7, "dumped": -0.7, "dumping": -0.7,
    "sell-off": -0.8, "selloff": -0.8, "sell off": -0.8, "selling": -0.4,
    "ban": -0.8, "bans": -0.8, "banned": -0.8, "banning": -0.7,
    "delist": -0.8, "delisted": -0.8, "delisting": -0.8,
    "breach": -0.8, "breached": -0.7, "liquidation": -0.6,
    "liquidations": -0.6, "liquidated": -0.7,
    # mild negative
    "slump": -0.6, "slumps": -0.6, "slumped": -0.5, "tumble": -0.6,
    "tumbles": -0.6, "tumbled": -0.5, "outflow": -0.5, "outflows": -0.5,
    "probe": -0.5, "probes": -0.4, "investigation": -0.5,
    "investigating": -0.4, "fud": -0.5, "downgrade": -0.6, "downgraded": -0.6,
    "reject": -0.6, "rejects": -0.6, "rejected": -0.6, "rejection": -0.5,
    "bear": -0.5, "bears": -0.4, "losses": -0.5, "loss": -0.4,
    "drop": -0.4, "drops": -0.4, "dropped": -0.4, "falls": -0.4,
    "fell": -0.4, "falling": -0.3, "decline": -0.4, "declines": -0.4,
    "warning": -0.4, "warns": -0.4, "warned": -0.3, "risk": -0.3,
    "risks": -0.3, "risky": -0.4, "delay": -0.3, "delayed": -0.3,
}

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("sentiment_analyzer")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scoring helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _compile_lexicon(lexicon: Dict[str, float]) -> List[Tuple[re.Pattern, float]]:
    r"""
    Pre-compile the keyword lexicon into STRICT word-boundary patterns.

    \b...\b guarantees e.g. 'ban' never matches inside 'bank' or
    'bankruptcy', and 'buy' never matches inside 'buying'.
    """
    return [
        (re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(keyword)}\b", re.IGNORECASE), weight)
        for keyword, weight in lexicon.items()
    ]


_BULLISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BULLISH_KEYWORDS)
_BEARISH_PATTERNS = _compile_lexicon(BEARISH_KEYWORDS)


def _base_asset(symbol: str) -> str:
    """'BTC/USDT' -> 'BTC'; strips exchange lot-size prefixes like '1000PEPE'."""
    base = symbol.split("/")[0].strip().upper()
    for prefix in ("1000000", "100000", "10000", "1000", "1M"):
        if base.startswith(prefix) and len(base) > len(prefix):
            return base[len(prefix):]
    return base


def score_headline(title: str) -> float:
    """Score a single headline title in [-1, 1] using the keyword lexicon."""
    score = 0.0
    for pattern, weight in _BULLISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    for pattern, weight in _BEARISH_PATTERNS:
        if pattern.search(title):
            score += weight
    return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))


def _aggregate(scores: Iterable[float]) -> float:
    """Mean of title scores, clipped to [-1, 1]; 0.0 when empty."""
    values = list(scores)
    if not values:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(values) / len(values))), 3)


def describe_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    """Human-readable label for a sentiment score."""
    if score >= SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bullish"
    if score <= -SENTIMENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "bearish"
    return "neutral"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 1: CryptoPanic free API (async title fetcher)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def fetch_cryptopanic_titles(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession, bases: List[str], api_key: str
) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """
    Fetch recent post titles from the CryptoPanic free API.

    Returns {base_asset: [title, ...]}. The API attributes each post to its
    currencies, so no keyword-based attribution is needed on this path.
    """
    params = {
        "auth_token": api_key,
        "currencies": ",".join(bases),
        "public": "true",
    }
    async with session.get(CRYPTOPANIC_API_URL, params=params) as resp:
        resp.raise_for_status()
        payload = await resp.json()

    titles: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    for post in payload.get("results", [])[:MAX_HEADLINES]:
        title = (post.get("title") or "").strip()
        if not title:
            continue
        for currency in post.get("currencies") or []:
            code = (currency.get("code") or "").upper()
            if code:
                titles.setdefault(code, []).append(title)

    logger.info(
        "CryptoPanic: %d titles fetched for %d coins",
        sum(len(v) for v in titles.values()), len(titles),
    )
    return titles


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Source 2: RSS fallback (async title fetcher + attribution)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def fetch_rss_titles(session: aiohttp.ClientSession) -> List[str]:
    """Fetch recent headlines from the fallback RSS feeds."""
    titles: List[str] = []
    for url in RSS_FEEDS:
        try:
            async with session.get(url) as resp:
                resp.raise_for_status()
                text = await resp.text()
            root = ET.fromstring(text)
            for item in root.iter("item"):
                title = (item.findtext("title") or "").strip()
                if title:
                    titles.append(title)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS feed unavailable (%s): %s", url, exc)
    logger.info("RSS fallback: %d titles fetched", len(titles))
    return titles[: MAX_HEADLINES * len(RSS_FEEDS)]


def _attribute_rss(
    titles: List[str], symbols: List[str]
) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """Attribute RSS titles to base assets by symbol / name mention."""
    per_coin: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    for symbol in symbols:
        base = _base_asset(symbol)
        tokens = {base.lower()}
        tokens.update(t for t in COIN_NAMES.get(base, "").lower().split() if t)
        for title in titles:
            if any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(t)}\b", title, re.IGNORECASE) for t in tokens):
                per_coin.setdefault(base, []).append(title)
    return per_coin


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def get_sentiment_scores(symbols: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Sentiment score in [-1, 1] for every symbol.

    Tries the CryptoPanic free API (when CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY is set), then
    the RSS fallback, and finally defaults every coin to 0.0 (neutral).
    This function never raises.
    """
    neutral = {symbol: 0.0 for symbol in symbols}
    if not symbols:
        return neutral

    bases = sorted({_base_asset(s) for s in symbols})
    api_key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    titles_per_coin: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    source = "no data (neutral default)"

    if api_key:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles_per_coin = await fetch_cryptopanic_titles(session, bases, api_key)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "CryptoPanic API"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("CryptoPanic API failed (%s) - trying RSS fallback", exc)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "%s not set - using RSS fallback (set the env var for better scores).",
            CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV,
        )

    if not titles_per_coin:
        try:
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT) as session:
                titles = await fetch_rss_titles(session)
            titles_per_coin = _attribute_rss(titles, symbols)
            if titles_per_coin:
                source = "RSS fallback"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("RSS fallback failed (%s) - defaulting to neutral.", exc)

    scores = {
        symbol: _aggregate(
            score_headline(t) for t in titles_per_coin.get(_base_asset(symbol), [])
        )
        for symbol in symbols
    }
    logger.info("Sentiment source: %s", source)
    logger.info("Sentiment scores: %s", {s: f"{v:+.2f}" for s, v in scores.items()})
    return scores


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Standalone smoke test
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async def _main() -> None:
    symbols = ["BTC/USDT", "ETH/USDT", "SOL/USDT", "XRP/USDT", "DOGE/USDT"]
    scores = await get_sentiment_scores(symbols)

    print("\n" + "=" * 62)
    print("TASK 5 SMOKE TEST - NEWS SENTIMENT")
    print("=" * 62)
    for symbol, score in scores.items():
        bar = "#" * int(round(abs(score) * 10))
        print(
            f"{symbol:<12} {score:+5.2f}  {bar:<10}  {describe_sentiment(score)}"
        )
    print("=" * 62)
    key = os.getenv(CRYPTOPANIC_KEY_ENV, "").strip()
    print(
        f"API key: {'configured (CryptoPanic API)' if key else 'NOT configured (RSS fallback / neutral)'}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        asyncio.run(_main())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterrupted by user.")
